2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Unified work queue**: All items (findings, subjective dimensions, synthesis stages, workflow actions) live in one queue
   - **Synthesis stages**: 4 sequential stages (`observe` → `reflect` → `organize` → `commit`) with dependency chain
   - **Dependency model**: `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` dict defines which stages must complete before others can start
   - **Interleave guardrail**: Prevents subjective items from starving objective items (3:1 burst pattern)
   - **Workflow kinds**: `workflow_stage` and `workflow_action` — synthetic queue items for planning workflow
   - **Plan lifecycle**: Synthesis stages are injected by `stale_dimensions.py` reconciliation when review finding snapshot hash changes
   - **query.json**: Machine-readable output consumed by agents; must include dependency state
   - **`--force-resolve`**: Escape hatch to override dependency blocking

3. Files and Code Sections:

     ```python
     def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
         """Separate synthetic IDs from real finding patterns."""
         synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
         remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
         return synthetic, remaining


     def _blocked_synthesis_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
         """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for synthesis stages that can't run yet."""
         from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES
         from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_IDS, SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS

         order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
         present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
         if not present:
             return {}

         confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {}).get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
         stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")

         blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
         for sid, name in zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
             if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
                 continue
             deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
             unmet = sorted(
                 f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
                 if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
             )
             if unmet:
                 blocked[sid] = unmet
         return blocked
     ```

     ```python
     synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
     if synthetic_ids:

         blocked_map = _blocked_synthesis_stages(plan)
         for sid in synthetic_ids:
             if sid in blocked_map:
                 deps = ", ".join(b.replace("synthesis::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
                 print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
                 print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
                 if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
                     return
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`** — Fix 4: Interleave guardrail
     - Note: This file was concurrently refactored by linter (plan_order functions extracted to separate module)
     - Added `_WORKFLOW_KINDS` frozenset and 3-way partition in `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()`
     ```python
     _WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})

     def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
         items: list[dict],
         *,
         objective_burst: int = 3,
         subjective_burst: int = 1,
     ) -> list[dict]:
         """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.

         Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
         interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
         """
         if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
             return items

         workflow_items: list[dict] = []
         subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
         objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
         for item in items:
             if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
                 workflow_items.append(item)
             elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
                 subjective_items.append(item)
             else:
                 objective_items.append(item)

         if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
             return items

         interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
         while objective_items and subjective_items:
             for _ in range(objective_burst):
                 if not objective_items:
                     break
                 interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
             for _ in range(subjective_burst):
                 if not subjective_items:
                     break
                 interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())

         interleaved.extend(objective_items)
         interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
         return interleaved
     ```

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Maximise the **strict score** honestly. Your main cycle: **scan → plan → execute → rescan**. Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    19→
    20→**Don't be lazy.** Do large refactors and small detailed fixes with equal energy. If it takes touching 20 files, touch 20 files. If it's a one-line change, make it. No task is too big or too small — fix things properly, not minimally.
    21→
    22→## 2. The Workflow
    23→
    24→Three phases, repeated as a cycle.
    25→
    26→### Phase 1: Scan and review — understand the codebase
    27→
    28→```bash
    29→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    30→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    31→```
    32→
    33→The scan will tell you if subjective dimensions need review. Follow its instructions. To trigger a review manually:
    34→```bash
    35→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    36→```
    37→
    38→### Phase 2: Plan — decide what to work on
    39→
    40→After reviews, synthesis stages and plan creation appear as queue items in `next`. Complete them in order:
    41→```bash
    42→desloppify next                                        # shows the next workflow step
    43→desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "themes and root causes..."
    44→desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."
    45→desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "summary of priorities..."
    46→desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "execution plan..."
    47→```
    48→
    49→Then shape the queue. **The plan shapes everything `next` gives you** — don't skip this step.
    50→
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                          # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top           # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>    # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>          # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan skip <pat>              # defer — hide from next
    57→```
    58→
    59→More plan commands:
    60→```bash
    61→desloppify plan move <cluster> top       # move all cluster members at once
    62→desloppify plan move <a> <b> top         # mix clusters + findings in one move
    63→desloppify plan move <pat> before -t X   # position relative to another item/cluster
    64→desloppify plan cluster move a,b top     # move multiple clusters as one block
    65→desloppify plan done <pat>               # mark complete
    66→desloppify plan reopen <pat>             # reopen
    67→```
    68→
    69→### Phase 3: Execute — grind the queue to completion
    70→
    71→Trust the plan and execute. Don't rescan mid-queue — finish the queue first.
    72→
    73→```
    74→1. desloppify next              ← what to fix next
    75→2. Fix the issue in code
    76→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    77→4. Repeat until the queue is empty
    78→```
    79→
    80→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    81→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    82→
    83→**When the queue is clear, go back to Phase 1.** New issues will surface, cascades will have resolved, priorities will have shifted. This is the cycle.
    84→
    85→### Other useful commands
    86→
    87→```bash
    88→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    89→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    90→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    91→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    92→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" --attest "..." # accept debt
    93→desloppify exclude <path>                         # exclude a directory from scanning
    94→desloppify config show                            # show all config including excludes
    95→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    96→```
    97→
    98→## 3. Reference
    99→
   100→### How scoring works
   101→
   102→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
   103→
   104→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
   105→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
   106→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
   107→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
   108→
   109→### Subjective reviews in detail
   110→
   111→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   112→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   113→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   114→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   115→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   116→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   117→
   118→### Key concepts
   119→
   120→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   121→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   122→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   123→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   124→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   125→
   126→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   127→
   128→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   129→
   130→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   131→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   132→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   133→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   134→
   135→## Prerequisite
   136→
   137→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   138→
   139→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   140→

> DEVELOPER

In the desloppify codebase at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify`, there's a problem with how blocked synthesis stages are ordered in the `next` command output.

When a user is in "plan mode" with all 4 synthesis stages pending (observe → reflect → organize → commit), `next` shows:
```
Next item  (#1 in queue)
(Planning stage: commit [blocked])
Planning: Write strategy & confirm
Blocked by: organize
Next step: desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize
```

This is confusing because:
- It shows the LAST stage (commit) as the top item, but it's blocked
- The actual first actionable stage (observe) is buried
- The "Next step" hint points to organize (the blocker of commit), not observe (the actual first thing to do)

Investigate:

1. Read `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` and find `build_synthesis_stage_items()` — how are synthesis items built and what sort keys do they get?

2. Read `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` — how does the interleave guardrail handle workflow items? Are blocked items sorted differently from unblocked ones?

3. Read `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` — look at `item_sort_key()` to understand how items are ranked.

The question: Why does `commit` (blocked) appear before `observe` (unblocked) in the queue? And what's the right fix — should blocked items sort AFTER unblocked ones? Or should `next` skip blocked items and show the first actionable one?

Provide a diagnosis and recommended fix with specific file/line references.

```
    44→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now

    49→    load_plan,

    53→
    54→
    55→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _has_synthesis_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Check if any synthesis stage ID (new or legacy) is in the queue."""
    60→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    61→    return bool(order & SYNTHESIS_IDS) or SYNTHESIS_ID in order
    62→
    63→
    64→def _inject_synthesis_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    65→    """Inject all 4 synthesis stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    66→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    67→    existing = set(order)
    68→    # Remove legacy ID if present
    69→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in existing:
    70→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
    71→    for sid in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS:
    72→        if sid not in existing:
    73→            order.insert(0 if sid == SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    74→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    75→    for sid in reversed(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS):
    76→        if sid in order:
    77→            order.remove(sid)
    78→    insert_at = 0
    79→    for sid in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS:
    80→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    81→        insert_at += 1
    82→
    83→
    84→def _purge_synthesis_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    85→    """Purge a single synthesis stage ID from the queue."""
    86→    sid = f"synthesis::{stage_name}"
    87→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    88→
    89→
    90→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    91→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    92→    try:
    93→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    94→    except ValueError:
    95→        return []
    96→    cleared: list[str] = []
    97→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    98→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    99→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
   100→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
```

```
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    51→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    52→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    53→    weight_by_confidence = {
    54→        "high": 1.0,
    55→        "medium": 0.7,
    56→        "low": 0.3,
    57→    }
    58→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    59→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    60→        weight *= 10.0
    61→    return float(weight)
    62→
    63→
    64→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    65→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    66→    if not scope:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    70→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    71→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    72→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    73→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    75→
    76→    if "*" in scope:
    77→        return any(
    78→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    79→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    80→        )
    81→
    82→    if "::" in scope:
    83→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    84→
    85→    lowered = scope.lower()
    86→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        return (
    88→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    89→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    90→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    91→        )
    92→
    93→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    94→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    95→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    96→
    97→    return (
    98→        detector == scope
    99→        or filepath == scope
   100→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   105→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   106→
   107→
   108→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   109→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   110→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   111→    target = cleaned.lower()
   112→
   113→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   114→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   115→            return str(dim_key)
   116→    return slugify(cleaned)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   120→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   121→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   122→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   123→    return {
   124→        cleaned.lower(),
   125→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   126→        slugify(cleaned),
   127→        canonical.lower(),
   128→        slugify(canonical),
   129→    }
   130→

   132→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   133→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   134→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   135→    if not lang:
   136→        return None
   137→
   138→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   140→        return None
   141→
   142→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   143→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   144→        return None
   145→
   146→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   147→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   148→        return None
   149→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}

   152→def primary_command_for_finding(
   153→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   154→) -> str:
   155→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   156→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   158→        available_fixers = [
   159→            fixer
   160→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   161→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   162→        ]
   163→        if available_fixers:
   164→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   165→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   166→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   167→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   168→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   169→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'

   172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   174→
   175→    Returns an empty list when no synthesis stages are pending.
   176→    """
   177→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
   178→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   179→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
   180→    )
   181→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   182→        SYNTHESIS_IDS,
   183→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→
   195→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   196→    open_review_count = sum(
   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   199→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({
   224→            "id": sid,
   225→            "tier": 1,
   226→            "confidence": "high",
   227→            "detector": "synthesis",
   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   230→            "stage_name": name,
   231→            "stage_index": idx,
   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   233→            "detail": {
   234→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items
   243→

   270→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   271→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   272→    if not dim_scores:
   273→        return {}
   274→
   275→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   276→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   277→    for entry in entries:
   278→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   279→        if not name:
   280→            continue
   281→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   282→        [REDACTED](name)
   283→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   284→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   285→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   286→            if not key:
   287→                continue
   288→            aliases.add(key)
   289→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   290→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   291→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   292→        for alias in aliases:
   293→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   294→    return scores
   295→
   296→
   297→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   298→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   299→
   300→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   301→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   302→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   303→    """
   304→    entries: list[dict] = []
   305→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   306→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   307→            continue
   308→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   309→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   310→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   311→            continue
   312→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   313→            continue
   314→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   315→        entries.append(
   316→            {
   317→                "name": name,
   318→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   319→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   320→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   321→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   322→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   323→                "placeholder": True,
   324→                "stale": False,
   325→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   326→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   327→            }
   328→        )
   329→    return entries
   330→
   331→
   332→def build_subjective_items(
   333→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   334→) -> list[dict]:
   335→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   336→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   337→    if not dim_scores:
   338→        return []
   339→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   340→
   341→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   342→
   343→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   344→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   345→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   346→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   347→        for e in subjective_entries
   348→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   349→    }
   350→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   351→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   352→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   353→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   354→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   355→
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   412→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   413→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   414→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   415→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   416→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   417→        if open_review > 0:
   418→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   419→        elif is_unassessed:
   420→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   421→        elif is_stale:
   422→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   423→                cli_keys,
   424→                force_review_rerun=True,
   425→            )
   426→        elif cli_keys:
   427→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   428→                cli_keys,
   429→                force_review_rerun=True,
   430→            )
   431→        else:
   432→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   433→                cli_keys,
   434→                force_review_rerun=True,
   435→            )
   436→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   437→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   438→        items.append(
   439→            {
   440→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   441→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   442→                "file": ".",
   443→                "confidence": "medium",
   444→                "summary": summary,
   445→                "detail": {
   446→                    "dimension_name": name,
   447→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   448→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   449→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   450→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   451→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   452→                },
   453→                "status": "open",
   454→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   455→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   456→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   457→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   458→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   459→            }
   460→        )
   461→    return items
   462→
   463→
   464→__all__ = [
   465→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   466→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   467→    "build_create_plan_item",
   468→    "build_subjective_items",
   469→    "build_synthesis_stage_items",
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from collections import deque
     7→from typing import TypedDict
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,
    14→    build_synthesis_stage_items,
    15→    scope_matches,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    18→    build_finding_items,
    19→    group_queue_items,
    20→    item_explain,
    21→    item_sort_key,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    24→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    25→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    26→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    29→
    30→
    31→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    32→class QueueBuildOptions:
    33→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    34→
    35→    count: int | None = 1
    36→    scan_path: str | None = None
    37→    scope: str | None = None
    38→    status: str = "open"
    39→    include_subjective: bool = True
    40→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    41→    chronic: bool = False
    42→    explain: bool = False
    43→    plan: dict | None = None
    44→    include_skipped: bool = False
    45→    cluster: str | None = None
    46→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    47→
    48→
    49→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    50→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    51→
    52→    items: list[dict]
    53→    total: int
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→    suppressed_count: int
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    60→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    61→
    62→
    63→_WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
    85→            workflow_items.append(item)
    86→        elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    87→            subjective_items.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            objective_items.append(item)
    90→
    91→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    92→        return items
    93→
    94→    interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
    95→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:
   102→                break
   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
   104→
   105→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   106→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
   107→    return interleaved
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_work_queue(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   114→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   115→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   116→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   117→
   118→    status = resolved_options.status
   119→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   120→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   121→    try:
   122→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   123→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   124→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   125→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   126→
   127→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   128→        state,
   129→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   130→        status_filter=status,
   131→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   132→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   136→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   137→    suppressed_count = 0
   138→    if (
   139→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   140→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   141→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   142→    ):
   143→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   144→            state,
   145→            state.get("findings", {}),
   146→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   147→        )
   148→        for item in subjective_items:
   149→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   150→                continue
   151→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   152→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   153→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   154→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   155→                suppressed_count += 1
   156→                continue
   157→            all_items.append(item)
   158→
   159→    # Inject synthesis stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   160→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   161→        synth_items = build_synthesis_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   162→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   163→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   164→        if plan_item is not None:
   165→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   166→
   167→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   168→
   169→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   170→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   171→    if resolved_options.plan:
   172→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   173→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   174→            all_items,
   175→            resolved_options.plan,
   176→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   177→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   178→            new_ids=new_ids,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   182→    should_collapse = (
   183→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   184→        and resolved_options.plan
   185→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   186→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   187→    )
   188→    if should_collapse:
   189→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   190→
   191→    should_interleave = (
   192→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   193→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   194→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   195→        and not resolved_options.scope
   196→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   197→    )
   198→    if should_interleave:
   199→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   200→
   201→    total = len(all_items)
   202→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   203→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   204→
   205→    if resolved_options.explain:
   206→        for item in all_items:
   207→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   208→
   209→    return {
   210→        "items": all_items,
   211→        "total": total,
   212→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   213→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   214→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→__all__ = [
   219→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   220→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   221→    "WorkQueueResult",
   222→    "build_work_queue",
   223→    "group_queue_items",
   224→]
   225→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    20→
    21→
    22→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    23→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    24→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    25→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    26→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    27→
    28→
    29→def build_finding_items(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    *,
    32→    scan_path: str | None,
    33→    status_filter: str,
    34→    scope: str | None,
    35→    chronic: bool,
    36→) -> list[dict]:
    37→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    38→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    39→    out: list[dict] = []
    40→
    41→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    45→            continue
    46→        if chronic and not (
    47→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    48→        ):
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        item = dict(finding)
    52→        item["id"] = finding_id
    53→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    54→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    55→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    56→        item["review_weight"] = (
    57→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    58→        )
    59→        subjective_score = None
    60→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    61→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    62→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    63→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    64→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    65→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    66→            )
    67→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    68→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    69→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    70→            item,
    71→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    72→        )
    73→
    74→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    75→            continue
    76→        out.append(item)
    77→
    78→    return out
    79→
    80→
    81→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    82→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    83→
    84→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    86→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    # Synthesis stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
    89→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
    90→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
    91→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
    92→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
    93→
    94→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
    95→    if kind == "workflow_action":
    96→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    97→
    98→    if kind == "cluster":
    99→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   100→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   101→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   102→        )
   103→        return (
   104→            0,
   105→            action_pri,
   106→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   111→        return (
   112→            1,
   113→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   114→            subjective_score_value(item),
   115→            item.get("id", ""),
   116→        )
   117→
   118→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   119→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   120→    return (
   121→        1,
   122→        0,
   123→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   124→        -review_weight,
   125→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   126→        item.get("id", ""),
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   131→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   132→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   133→        return {
   134→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   135→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   136→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   137→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   138→            "policy": "Synthesis stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   139→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   140→        }
   141→
   142→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   143→        return {
   144→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   145→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after synthesis stages, before findings.",
   146→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   147→        }
   148→
   149→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   150→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   151→        return {
   152→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   153→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   154→            "initial_review": initial,
   155→            "policy": (
   156→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   157→                if initial else
   158→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   159→            ),
   160→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   161→        }
   162→
   163→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   164→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   165→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   166→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   167→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   168→    if is_subjective:
   169→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   170→    elif is_review:
   171→        ranking_factors = [
   172→            "confidence asc",
   173→            "review_weight desc",
   174→            "count desc",
   175→            "id asc",
   176→        ]
   177→    else:
   178→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   179→    explain = {
   180→        "kind": "finding",
   181→        "confidence": confidence,
   182→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   183→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   184→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   185→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   186→    }
   187→    if is_review:
   188→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   189→    if is_subjective:
   190→        explain["policy"] = (
   191→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   192→        )
   193→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   194→    return explain
   195→
   196→
   197→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   198→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   199→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   200→    for item in items:
   201→        if group == "file":
   202→            key = item.get("file", "")
   203→        elif group == "detector":
   204→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   205→        elif group == "cluster":
   206→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   207→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   208→        else:
   209→            key = "items"
   210→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   211→    return grouped
   212→
   213→
   214→__all__ = [
   215→    "build_finding_items",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "subjective_score_value",
   219→    "group_queue_items",
   220→]
   221→
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     7→
     8→
     9→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    10→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    11→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    12→    if not scan_history:
    13→        return set()
    14→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    15→    if not threshold:
    16→        return set()
    17→    return {
    18→        finding_id
    19→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    20→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def apply_plan_order(
    25→    items: list[dict],
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    29→    cluster: str | None = None,
    30→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    33→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    34→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    35→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    36→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    37→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    38→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    39→
    40→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for item in items:
    42→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    43→
    44→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    45→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    46→        if override.get("description"):
    47→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    48→        if override.get("note"):
    49→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    50→        if override.get("cluster"):
    51→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    52→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    53→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    54→                "name": cluster_name,
    55→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    56→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    57→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    58→            }
    59→
    60→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    61→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    62→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    63→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    64→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    65→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    66→
    67→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    68→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    69→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    70→    _new = new_ids or set()
    71→    for item in items:
    72→        item_id = item["id"]
    73→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    74→            continue
    75→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    76→            skipped_items.append(item)
    77→        elif item_id in _new:
    78→            remaining_new.append(item)
    79→        else:
    80→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    81→
    82→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    83→    if include_skipped:
    84→        result = result + skipped_items
    85→
    86→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
    87→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
    88→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    89→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
    90→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
    91→            if skip_entry:
    92→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
    93→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
    94→                if skip_reason:
    95→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    96→
    97→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
    98→    if effective_cluster:
    99→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   100→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   101→        if cluster_member_ids:
   102→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   103→    return result
   104→
   105→
   106→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   107→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   108→    try:
   109→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   110→
   111→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   112→        if meta:
   113→            return meta.action_type
   114→    except ImportError as exc:
   115→        _ = exc
   116→    return "manual_fix"
   117→
   118→
   119→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   120→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   121→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   122→    if not clusters:
   123→        return items
   124→
   125→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   126→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   127→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   128→            continue
   129→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   130→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   131→
   132→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   133→        return items
   134→
   135→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   136→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   137→    for item in items:
   138→        cluster_name = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   139→        if cluster_name:
   140→            cluster_members.setdefault(cluster_name, []).append(item)
   141→        else:
   142→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   143→
   144→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   145→    for cluster_name, members in cluster_members.items():
   146→        if len(members) < 2:
   147→            result.extend(members)
   148→            continue
   149→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   150→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   151→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   152→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   153→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   154→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   155→            action_type = "reorganize"
   156→        else:
   157→            action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   158→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   159→                action_type = "refactor"
   160→
   161→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   162→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   163→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   164→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   165→        else:
   166→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   167→
   168→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   169→        if not primary_command:
   170→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   171→
   172→        meta_item: dict = {
   173→            "id": cluster_name,
   174→            "kind": "cluster",
   175→            "action_type": action_type,
   176→            "summary": summary,
   177→            "members": members,
   178→            "member_count": len(members),
   179→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   180→            "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   181→            "cluster_auto": True,
   182→            "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   183→            "confidence": "high",
   184→            "detector": detector,
   185→            "file": "",
   186→        }
   187→        result.append(meta_item)
   188→
   189→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   190→    return result
   191→
   192→
   193→__all__ = ["apply_plan_order", "collapse_clusters", "new_item_ids"]
   194→
   195→
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    19→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    20→        "file": member.get("file"),
    21→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    22→        "status": member.get("status"),
    23→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    24→    }
    25→
    26→
    27→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    28→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    29→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    30→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    31→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    32→        serialized_members = [
    33→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    34→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    35→        ]
    36→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    37→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    38→            "id": item.get("id"),
    39→            "kind": "cluster",
    40→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    41→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    42→            "member_count": member_count,
    43→            "members": serialized_members,
    44→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    45→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    46→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    47→        }
    48→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    52→        return serialized_cluster
    53→
    54→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    55→        "id": item.get("id"),
    56→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    57→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    58→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    59→        "file": item.get("file"),
    60→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    61→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    62→        "status": item.get("status"),
    63→    }
    64→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    65→
    66→    # Workflow dependency state
    67→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    68→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    69→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    70→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    71→
    72→    explain = item.get("explain")
    73→    if explain is not None:
    74→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    75→
    76→    # Plan metadata
    77→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    78→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    79→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    80→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    81→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    82→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    83→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    84→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    85→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    86→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    87→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    88→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    89→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    90→
    91→    return serialized
    92→
    93→
    94→def build_query_payload(
    95→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    96→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    97→    *,
    98→    command: str,
    99→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   100→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   101→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   102→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   103→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   104→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   105→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   106→        "suppressed_count": queue.get("suppressed_count", 0),
   107→    }
   108→
   109→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   110→        "command": command,
   111→        "items": serialized,
   112→        "queue": queue_section,
   113→        "narrative": narrative,
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    if plan and (
   117→        plan.get("queue_order")
   118→        or plan.get("skipped")
   119→        or plan.get("clusters")
   120→    ):
   121→        clusters_summary = []
   122→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   123→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   124→            clusters_summary.append({
   125→                "name": name,
   126→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   127→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   128→            })
   129→        payload["plan"] = {
   130→            "active": True,
   131→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   132→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   133→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   134→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   135→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   136→        }
   137→
   138→    return payload
   139→
   140→
   141→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   142→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   143→    lines = [
   144→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   145→        "",
   146→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   147→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   148→    ]
   149→    for item in items:
   150→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   151→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   152→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   153→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   154→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   155→    lines.append("")
   156→    return "\n".join(lines)
   157→
   158→
   159→def write_output_file(
   160→    output_file: str,
   161→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   162→    item_count: int,
   163→    *,
   164→    safe_write_text_fn,
   165→    colorize_fn,
   166→) -> bool:
   167→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   168→    try:
   169→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   170→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   171→    except OSError as exc:
   172→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   173→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   174→        return False
   175→    return True
   176→
   177→
   178→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   179→    output_format: str,
   180→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   181→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   182→) -> bool:
   183→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   184→    renderers = {
   185→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   186→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   187→    }
   188→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   189→    if renderer is None:
   190→        return False
   191→    renderer()
   192→    return True
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = [
   196→    "build_query_payload",
   197→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   198→    "render_markdown",
   199→    "serialize_item",
   200→    "write_output_file",
   201→]
   202→
```
