> AGENT

Now I have the full diagnosis. Here is the comprehensive answer.

---

## Diagnosis

### Summary

The bug is that `apply_plan_order()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` (lines 60-64) blindly follows `queue_order` without respecting synthesis stage dependency constraints. When `queue_order` has synthesis IDs in the wrong order, blocked stages appear before unblocked ones in the final output.

### Detailed Trace

The pipeline for `next` builds items through these stages in `build_work_queue()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`:

1. **Line 167** -- `all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)` -- This sort is CORRECT. In `item_sort_key()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` lines 89-92, synthesis stages get sort key `(-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, id)`. Unblocked `observe` gets `(-2, 0, 0, ...)` which sorts before blocked `commit` at `(-2, 1, 3, ...)`.

2. **Line 173** -- `all_items = _apply_plan_order(...)` -- This OVERRIDES the correct sort. `apply_plan_order()` at `plan_order.py` lines 60-64 iterates `queue_order` and emits items in that exact order. If `queue_order` has `commit` before `observe`, the correct `item_sort_key` ordering is destroyed.

3. **Lines 188-189** -- `collapse_clusters()` -- This would re-sort with `item_sort_key` (fixing the order), but it only runs when auto-clusters exist to collapse. With no auto-clusters, it returns items unchanged.

4. **Lines 198-199** -- `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()` -- This extracts workflow items and prepends them, preserving their order from step 2. It only runs when both subjective AND objective items exist. If either is missing, it returns items unchanged.

5. **Line 202** -- `all_items[:count]` -- With default `count=1`, the first item becomes the `next` output.

### How `queue_order` Gets Wrong

There are multiple ways:

**Direct reordering** -- `move_items()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py` lines 151-190 has no guard preventing synthesis stage reordering. A user running `plan move synthesis::commit top` would place `commit` first:

```python
# After: plan move synthesis::commit top
queue_order = ['synthesis::commit', 'synthesis::observe', 'synthesis::reflect', 'synthesis::organize', ...]
```

**Wildcard pattern matching** -- If a user does `plan move synthesis::* top`, the resolved IDs might come back in an arbitrary order depending on how `resolve_ids_from_patterns` iterates findings.

I verified this by reproducing it:

```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import move_items
move_items(plan, ['synthesis::commit'], 'top')
# Result: ['synthesis::commit', 'synthesis::observe', 'synthesis::reflect', 'synthesis::organize', ...]
```

Then through `apply_plan_order`, the final queue has `commit` (blocked) at position 1 and `observe` (unblocked) at position 4.

### Why No Downstream Fix Kicks In

The two mechanisms that could correct the ordering both have conditions that prevent them from firing:

- `collapse_clusters` (line 189 of `core.py`) only triggers when there are auto-clusters with 2+ members. No clusters means no re-sort.
- `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` (line 199) only triggers when both subjective and objective queues are non-empty AND no scope/cluster filter is set. During pure synthesis mode (only synthesis items in queue), or when scope is set, it early-returns unchanged.

## Recommended Fix

The right fix is to **enforce dependency order for synthesis stages within `apply_plan_order()`**, since their dependency constraints are hard invariants that should not be overridden by plan ordering. This is analogous to a topological sort constraint: regardless of what the user requested, a blocked stage must come after the stage that unblocks it.

### Implementation

In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`, add a post-processing step after the `ordered` list is built (after line 64) that re-sorts synthesis stage items by their `blocked_penalty` and `stage_index`:

```python
def apply_plan_order(
    items: list[dict],
    plan: dict,
    *,
    include_skipped: bool = False,
    cluster: str | None = None,
    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")

    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for item in items:
        by_id[item["id"]] = item

    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
        if override.get("description"):
            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
        if override.get("note"):
            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
        if override.get("cluster"):
            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
            item["plan_cluster"] = {
                "name": cluster_name,
                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
            }

    ordered: list[dict] = []
    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    for finding_id in queue_order:
        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)

    # --- NEW: enforce dependency order for workflow stages ---
    _enforce_workflow_stage_order(ordered)

    # ... rest of the function unchanged ...
```

And add the helper function:

```python
def _enforce_workflow_stage_order(items: list[dict]) -> None:
    """Re-sort workflow_stage items in-place by (blocked_penalty, stage_index).

    This ensures blocked synthesis stages always appear after unblocked ones,
    regardless of their position in queue_order.  Non-workflow items keep
    their positions.
    """
    # Collect indices and items for workflow stages
    stage_entries: list[tuple[int, dict]] = []
    for i, item in enumerate(items):
        if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage":
            stage_entries.append((i, item))

    if len(stage_entries) <= 1:
        return

    # Sort by blocked_penalty then stage_index (matching item_sort_key)
    sorted_stages = sorted(
        [item for _, item in stage_entries],
        key=lambda it: (1 if it.get("is_blocked") else 0, int(it.get("stage_index", 0))),
    )

    # Write back into original positions
    for (original_idx, _), replacement in zip(stage_entries, sorted_stages):
        items[original_idx] = replacement
```

This fix:

1. **Only affects workflow_stage items** -- regular finding ordering from `queue_order` is preserved exactly as before.
2. **Keeps workflow stages in their original positions relative to other items** -- if the user positioned synthesis items between findings, they stay between those findings. Only the internal ordering among synthesis stages is corrected.
3. **Respects the blocked/unblocked invariant** -- unblocked stages always appear before blocked ones.
4. **Is self-contained** -- no changes needed in `item_sort_key`, `build_synthesis_stage_items`, or the interleave guardrail.

### Secondary Fix (Defense in Depth)

Additionally, `move_items()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py` line 151 should reject or warn when the user tries to move synthesis stage IDs, since their order is determined by their dependency chain:

```python
def move_items(plan, finding_ids, position, target=None, offset=None):
    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_IDS
    
    synth_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid in SYNTHESIS_IDS]
    if synth_ids:
        # Could either reject the move or silently skip synthesis IDs
        finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in SYNTHESIS_IDS]
        if not finding_ids:
            return 0  # Nothing to move
    # ... rest of function
```

This prevents the queue_order from getting into a bad state in the first place, while the `_enforce_workflow_stage_order` fix handles any existing bad state.

   123→        "--validate-import",
   124→        dest="validate_import_file",
   125→        type=str,
   126→        metavar="FILE",
   127→        help="Validate review import payload and selected trust mode without mutating state",
   128→    )
   129→    g_core.add_argument(
   130→        "--allow-partial",
   131→        action="store_true",
   132→        help=(
   133→            "Allow partial review import when invalid findings are skipped "
   134→            "(default: fail on any skipped finding)"
   135→        ),
   136→    )
   137→    g_core.add_argument(
   138→        "--dimensions",
   139→        type=str,
   140→        default=None,
   141→        help="Comma-separated dimensions to evaluate",
   142→    )
   143→    g_core.add_argument(
   144→        "--retrospective",
   145→        action="store_true",
   146→        help=(
   147→            "Include historical review issue status/note context in the packet "
   148→            "to support root-cause vs symptom analysis during review"
   149→        ),
   150→    )
   151→    g_core.add_argument(
   152→        "--retrospective-max-issues",
   153→        type=int,
   154→        default=30,
   155→        help="Max recent historical issues to include in review context (default: 30)",
   156→    )
   157→    g_core.add_argument(
   158→        "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   159→        type=int,
   160→        default=20,
   161→        help="Max history items included per batch focus slice (default: 20)",
   162→    )
   163→    g_core.add_argument(
   164→        "--force-review-rerun",
   165→        action="store_true",
   166→        help="Bypass the objective-plan-drained gate for review reruns",
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    # -- external review --
   170→    g_external = p_review.add_argument_group("external review")
   171→    g_external.add_argument(
   172→        "--external-start",
   173→        action="store_true",
   174→        help=(
   175→            "Start a cloud external review session (generates blind packet, "
   176→            "session id/token, and reviewer template)"
   177→        ),
   178→    )
   179→    g_external.add_argument(
   180→        "--external-submit",
   181→        action="store_true",
   182→        help=(
   183→            "Submit external reviewer JSON via a started session; "
   184→            "CLI injects canonical provenance before import"
   185→        ),
   186→    )
   187→    g_external.add_argument(
   188→        "--session-id",
   189→        type=str,
   190→        default=None,
   191→        help="External review session id for --external-submit",
   192→    )
   193→    g_external.add_argument(
   194→        "--external-runner",
   195→        choices=["claude"],
   196→        default="claude",
   197→        help="External reviewer runner for --external-start (default: claude)",
   198→    )
   199→    g_external.add_argument(
   200→        "--session-ttl-hours",
   201→        type=int,
   202→        default=24,
   203→        help="External review session expiration in hours (default: 24)",
   204→    )
   205→
   206→    # -- batch execution --
   207→    g_batch = p_review.add_argument_group("batch execution")
   208→    g_batch.add_argument(
   209→        "--run-batches",
   210→        action="store_true",
   211→        help="Run holistic investigation batches with subagents and merge/import output",
   212→    )
   213→    g_batch.add_argument(
   214→        "--runner",
   215→        choices=["codex"],
   216→        default="codex",
   217→        help="Subagent runner backend (default: codex)",
   218→    )
   219→    g_batch.add_argument(
   220→        "--parallel", action="store_true", help="Run selected batches in parallel"
   221→    )
   222→    g_batch.add_argument(
   223→        "--max-parallel-batches",
   224→        type=int,
   225→        default=3,
   226→        help=(
   227→            "Max concurrent subagent batches when --parallel is enabled "
   228→            "(default: 3)"
   229→        ),
   230→    )
   231→    g_batch.add_argument(
   232→        "--batch-timeout-seconds",
   233→        type=int,
   234→        default=20 * 60,
   235→        help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
   236→    )
   237→    g_batch.add_argument(
   238→        "--batch-max-retries",
   239→        type=int,
   240→        default=1,
   241→        help=(
   242→            "Retries per failed batch for transient runner/network errors "
   243→            "(default: 1)"
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    g_batch.add_argument(
   247→        "--batch-retry-backoff-seconds",
   248→        type=float,
   249→        default=2.0,
   250→        help=(
   251→            "Base backoff delay for transient batch retries in seconds "
   252→            "(default: 2.0)"
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→    g_batch.add_argument(
   256→        "--batch-heartbeat-seconds",
   257→        type=float,
   258→        default=15.0,
   259→        help=(
   260→            "Progress heartbeat interval during parallel batch runs in seconds "
   261→            "(default: 15.0)"
   262→        ),
   263→    )
   264→    g_batch.add_argument(
   265→        "--batch-stall-warning-seconds",
   266→        type=int,
   267→        default=0,
   268→        help=(
   269→            "Emit warning when a running batch exceeds this elapsed time "
   270→            "(0 disables warnings; does not terminate the batch)"
   271→        ),
   272→    )
   273→    g_batch.add_argument(
   274→        "--batch-stall-kill-seconds",
   275→        type=int,
   276→        default=120,
   277→        help=(
   278→            "Terminate a batch when output state is unchanged and runner streams are idle "
   279→            "for this many seconds (default: 120; 0 disables kill recovery)"
   280→        ),
   281→    )
   282→    g_batch.add_argument(
   283→        "--dry-run",
   284→        action="store_true",
   285→        help="Generate packet/prompts only (skip runner/import)",
   286→    )
   287→    g_batch.add_argument(
   288→        "--run-log-file",
   289→        type=str,
   290→        default=None,
   291→        help=(
   292→            "Optional explicit path for live run log output "
   293→            "(overrides default run artifacts path)"
   294→        ),
   295→    )
   296→    g_batch.add_argument(
   297→        "--packet",
   298→        type=str,
   299→        default=None,
   300→        help="Use an existing immutable packet JSON instead of preparing a new one",
   301→    )
   302→    g_batch.add_argument(
   303→        "--only-batches",
   304→        type=str,
   305→        default=None,
   306→        help="Comma-separated 1-based batch indexes to run (e.g. 1,3,5)",
   307→    )
   308→    g_batch.add_argument(
   309→        "--scan-after-import",
   310→        action="store_true",
   311→        help="Run `scan` after successful merged import",
   312→    )
   313→    g_batch.add_argument(
   314→        "--import-run",
   315→        dest="import_run_dir",
   316→        type=str,
   317→        metavar="DIR",
   318→        default=None,
   319→        help=(
   320→            "Re-import results from a completed run directory "
   321→            "(replays merge+import when the original pipeline was interrupted)"
   322→        ),
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    # -- trust & attestation --
   326→    g_trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
   327→    g_trust.add_argument(
   328→        "--manual-override",
   329→        action="store_true",
   330→        help=(
   331→            "Allow untrusted assessment score imports. Findings always import; "
   332→            "scores require trusted blind provenance unless this override is set."
   333→        ),
   334→    )
   335→    g_trust.add_argument(
   336→        "--attested-external",
   337→        action="store_true",
   338→        help=(
   339→            "Accept external blind-run assessments as durable scores when "
   340→            "paired with --attest and valid blind packet provenance "
   341→            "(intended for cloud Claude subagent workflows)."
   342→        ),
   343→    )
   344→    g_trust.add_argument(
   345→        "--attest",
   346→        type=str,
   347→        default=None,
   348→        help=(
   349→            "Required with --manual-override or --attested-external. "
   350→            "For attested external imports include both phrases "
   351→            "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'."
   352→        ),
   353→    )
   354→
   355→    # -- post-processing --
   356→    g_post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
   357→    g_post.add_argument(
   358→        "--merge",
   359→        action="store_true",
   360→        help="Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings",
   361→    )
   362→    g_post.add_argument(
   363→        "--similarity",
   364→        type=float,
   365→        default=0.8,
   366→        help="Summary similarity threshold for merge (0-1, default: 0.8)",
   367→    )
   368→
   369→    # -- deprecated --
   370→    g_deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated")
   371→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   372→        "--max-age",
   373→        type=int,
   374→        default=None,
   375→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   376→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   377→    )
   378→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   379→        "--max-files",
   380→        type=int,
   381→        default=None,
   382→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   383→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   384→    )
   385→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   386→        "--refresh",
   387→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   388→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   389→    )
   390→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   391→        "--holistic",
   392→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   393→        help="Deprecated: holistic is now the only review mode",
   394→    )
   395→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   396→        "--save-run-log",
   397→        action="store_true",
   398→        help=(
   399→            "Deprecated no-op: run logs are now always saved while running "
   400→            "(default location: run artifacts dir)"
   401→        ),
   402→    )

   405→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   406→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   407→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   408→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   409→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   410→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   411→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   412→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   413→    z_set.add_argument(
   414→        "zone_value",
   415→        type=str,
   416→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   417→    )
   418→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   419→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   420→
   421→
   422→def _add_config_parser(sub) -> None:
   423→    p_config = sub.add_parser("config", help="Show/set/unset project configuration")
   424→    config_sub = p_config.add_subparsers(dest="config_action")
   425→    config_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show all config values")
   426→    c_set = config_sub.add_parser("set", help="Set a config value")
   427→    c_set.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   428→    c_set.add_argument("config_value", type=str, help="Value to set")
   429→    c_unset = config_sub.add_parser("unset", help="Reset a config key to default")
   430→    c_unset.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   431→
   432→
   433→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   434→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   435→    for lang_name in langs:
   436→        try:
   437→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   438→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   439→            fixer_names = []
   440→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   441→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   442→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   443→    return fixer_help_lines
   444→
   445→
   446→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   447→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   448→        "fix",
   449→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   450→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   451→    )
   452→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   453→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   454→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   455→    p_fix.add_argument(
   456→        "--dry-run",
   457→        action="store_true",
   458→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   459→    )
   460→
   461→
   462→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   463→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   464→        "plan",
   465→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
   466→        description="""\
   467→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   468→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
   469→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   470→        epilog="""\
   471→typical workflow:
   472→  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
   473→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   474→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   475→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
   476→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   477→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   478→  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   479→
   480→patterns (used by move, skip, done, describe, note, etc.):
   481→  Patterns match findings by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
   482→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
   483→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
   484→
   485→subcommands:
   486→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   487→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   488→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   489→  move       Move findings or clusters in the queue
   490→  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   491→  describe   Set augmented description
   492→  note       Set note on findings
   493→  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   494→  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
   495→  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
   496→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   497→  cluster    Manage finding clusters
   498→  synthesize Staged synthesis workflow (after review)""",
   499→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   500→    )
   501→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   502→    p_plan.add_argument(
   503→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
   504→    )
   505→
   506→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   507→
   508→    # plan show
   509→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   510→
   511→    # plan queue
   512→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
   513→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
   514→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
   515→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
   516→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
   517→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
   518→    p_queue.add_argument("--sort", choices=["priority", "recent"], default="priority",
   519→                         help="Sort order (default: priority)")
   520→
   521→    # plan reset
   522→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   523→
   524→    # plan move <patterns> <position> [--target TARGET]
   525→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
   526→        "move",
   527→        help="Move findings in the queue",
   528→        epilog="""\
   529→patterns accept finding IDs, detector names, file paths, globs, or cluster names.
   530→cluster names expand to all member IDs automatically.
   531→
   532→examples:
   533→  desloppify plan move security top                         # all findings from detector
   534→  desloppify plan move "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top          # glob pattern
   535→  desloppify plan move smells bottom                        # deprioritize
   536→  desloppify plan move my-cluster top                       # cluster members
   537→  desloppify plan move my-cluster unused top                # mix clusters + findings
   538→  desloppify plan move unused before -t security            # before a finding/cluster
   539→  desloppify plan move smells after -t my-cluster           # after a cluster
   540→  desloppify plan move security up -t 3                     # shift up 3 positions""",
   541→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   542→    )
   543→    p_move.add_argument(
   544→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   545→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   546→    )
   547→    p_move.add_argument(
   548→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   549→        help="Where to move",
   550→    )
   551→    p_move.add_argument(
   552→        "-t", "--target", default=None,
   553→        help="Required for before/after (finding ID or cluster name) and up/down (integer offset)",
   554→    )
   555→
   556→    # plan describe <patterns> "<text>"
   557→    p_describe = plan_sub.add_parser("describe", help="Set augmented description")
   558→    p_describe.add_argument(
   559→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   560→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   561→    )
   562→    p_describe.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Description text")
   563→
   564→    # plan note <patterns> "<text>"
   565→    p_note = plan_sub.add_parser("note", help="Set note on findings")
   566→    p_note.add_argument(
   567→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   568→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   569→    )
   570→    p_note.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Note text")
   571→
   572→    # plan skip <patterns> [--reason] [--review-after N] [--permanent] [--false-positive] [--note] [--attest]
   573→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   574→        "skip",
   575→        help="Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent (wontfix), or --false-positive",
   576→    )
   577→    p_skip.add_argument(
   578→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   579→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   580→    )
   581→    p_skip.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default=None, help="Why this is being skipped")
   582→    p_skip.add_argument(
   583→        "--review-after", type=int, default=None, metavar="N",
   584→        help="Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)",
   585→    )
   586→    p_skip.add_argument(
   587→        "--permanent", action="store_true",
   588→        help="Mark as wontfix (score-affecting, requires --note and --attest)",
   589→    )
   590→    p_skip.add_argument(
   591→        "--false-positive", action="store_true",
   592→        help="Mark as false positive (requires --attest)",
   593→    )
   594→    p_skip.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation (required for --permanent)")
   595→    p_skip.add_argument(
   596→        "--attest", type=str, default=None,
   597→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",
   598→    )
   599→
   600→    # plan unskip <patterns>
   601→    p_unskip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   602→        "unskip", help="Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens permanent/fp in state)"
   603→    )
   604→    p_unskip.add_argument(
   605→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   606→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   607→    )
   608→
   609→    # plan reopen <patterns>
   610→    p_reopen = plan_sub.add_parser(
   611→        "reopen", help="Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue"
   612→    )
   613→    p_reopen.add_argument(
   614→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   615→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   616→    )
   617→
   618→    # plan done <patterns> --attest [--note]
   619→    p_done = plan_sub.add_parser(
   620→        "done",
   621→        help="Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next step)",
   622→        epilog="""\
   623→examples:
   624→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
   625→    --attest "I have actually removed the import and I am not gaming the score."
   626→  desloppify plan done security --note "patched XSS" \\
   627→    --attest "I have actually ..."  """,
   628→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   629→    )
   630→    p_done.add_argument(
   631→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   632→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   633→    )
   634→    p_done.add_argument(
   635→        "--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation of the fix"
   636→    )
   637→    p_done.add_argument(
   638→        "--attest",
   639→        type=str,
   640→        default=None,
   641→        help=(
   642→            "Required anti-gaming attestation. Must include BOTH keywords "
   643→            "'I have actually' and 'not gaming'."
   644→        ),
   645→    )
   646→    p_done.add_argument(
   647→        "--confirm",
   648→        action="store_true",
   649→        default=False,
   650→        help="Auto-generate attestation from --note (requires --note)",
   651→    )
   652→    p_done.add_argument(
   653→        "--force-resolve",
   654→        action="store_true",
   655→        default=False,
   656→        dest="force_resolve",
   657→        help="Bypass synthesis guardrail when new findings are pending triage",
   658→    )
   659→
   660→    # plan focus <cluster> | --clear
   661→    p_focus = plan_sub.add_parser("focus", help="Set or clear active cluster focus")
   662→    p_focus.add_argument("cluster_name", nargs="?", default=None, help="Cluster name")
   663→    p_focus.add_argument("--clear", action="store_true", help="Clear focus")
   664→
   665→    # plan cluster ...
   666→    p_cluster = plan_sub.add_parser(
   667→        "cluster",
   668→        help="Manage finding clusters",
   669→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   670→    )
   671→    cluster_sub = p_cluster.add_subparsers(dest="cluster_action")
   672→
   673→    # plan cluster create <name> [--description "..."] [--action "..."]
   674→    p_cc = cluster_sub.add_parser("create", help="Create a cluster")
   675→    p_cc.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name (slug)")
   676→    p_cc.add_argument("--description", type=str, default=None, help="Cluster description")
   677→    p_cc.add_argument("--action", type=str, default=None, help="Primary action/command for this cluster")
   678→
   679→    # plan cluster add <cluster> <patterns...>
   680→    p_ca = cluster_sub.add_parser("add", help="Add findings to a cluster")

    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:
    75→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→
    81→
    82→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    83→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    84→    plan = load_plan()
    85→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    86→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    87→    reset_plan(plan)
    88→    append_log_entry(
    89→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    90→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    91→    )
    92→    save_plan(plan)
    93→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    97→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    98→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    99→
   100→    if plan_action is None:
   101→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "show":
   105→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action == "queue":
   109→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "reset":
   113→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    if plan_action == "move":
   117→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action in (
   121→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   122→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   123→    ):
   124→        dispatch = {
   125→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   126→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   127→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   128→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   129→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   130→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   131→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   132→        }
   133→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   137→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   138→        return
   139→
   140→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   141→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   142→
   143→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   147→
   148→
   149→# Backwards-compatible alias
   150→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   151→
   152→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""

    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    41→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    42→
    43→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    44→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    45→    """
    46→    cited: set[str] = set()
    47→    # Match full finding IDs
    48→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    49→        candidate = match.group()
    50→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    51→            cited.add(candidate)
    52→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    53→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    54→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    55→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    56→                cited.add(valid_id)
    57→                break
    58→    return cited
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# Completed-work helpers
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    66→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    67→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    68→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    69→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    70→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    71→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    72→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    73→                if ts:
    74→                    return str(ts)
    75→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    76→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    77→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    82→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    83→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    84→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    85→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    88→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    89→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    90→    """
    91→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    92→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    93→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    94→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    98→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    99→        dim = _dimension(f)

    25→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, log
    26→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    27→from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown, compute_score_impact
    28→
    29→def _render_item(
    30→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
    31→    potentials: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
    34→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    35→        _render_cluster_item(item)
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    # Workflow stage item (synthesis stages)
    39→    if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage":
    40→        blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    41→        stage = item.get("stage_name", "")
    42→        tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    43→        style = "dim" if blocked else "bold"
    44→        print(colorize(f"  (Planning stage: {stage}{tag})", style))
    45→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    46→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    47→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
    48→        total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
    49→        if total:
    50→            print(colorize(f"  {total} review findings to analyze", "dim"))
    51→        if blocked:
    52→            blocked_by = item.get("blocked_by", [])
    53→            deps = ", ".join(b.replace("synthesis::", "") for b in blocked_by)
    54→            print(colorize(f"  Blocked by: {deps}", "dim"))
    55→            # Show the unblocking action
    56→            first_dep = blocked_by[0] if blocked_by else ""
    57→            dep_name = first_dep.replace("synthesis::", "")
    58→            if dep_name:
    59→                print(colorize(f"  Next step: desloppify plan synthesize --stage {dep_name}", "dim"))
    60→        else:
    61→            print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', '')}", "cyan"))
    62→        return
    63→
    64→    # Workflow action item (e.g. create-plan)
    65→    if item.get("kind") == "workflow_action":
    66→        print(colorize("  (Workflow step)", "bold"))
    67→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    68→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    69→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', '')}", "cyan"))
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    73→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))

    82→

    84→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    85→        detail = item.get("detail", {})

    89→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
    90→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
    91→        print(

    93→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",

    95→            )
    96→        )
    97→        print(colorize(

   100→        ))
   101→        if explain:

   106→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   110→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   111→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   112→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   113→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   114→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   115→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   116→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   117→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   118→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))

   123→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   124→
   125→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   126→    if isinstance(detail, str):
   127→        detail = {"suggestion": detail}
   128→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
   129→        detail.setdefault("lines", [])
   130→        detail.setdefault("line", None)
   131→        detail.setdefault("category", None)
   132→        detail.setdefault("importers", None)
   133→        detail.setdefault("count", 0)
   134→    if detail.get("lines"):
   135→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   136→    if detail.get("category"):
   137→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   138→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:

   140→    if detail.get("suggestion"):

   142→
   143→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   144→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   145→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   146→        if snippet:

   148→            print(snippet)
   149→
   150→    if dim_scores:
   151→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   152→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   153→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   154→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   155→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   156→            print(
   157→                colorize(

   159→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   160→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   161→                    "dim",
   162→                )
   163→            )
   164→
   165→    # Score impact estimate
   166→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   167→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   168→        try:
   169→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   170→            if impact > 0:
   171→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   172→            else:
   173→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   174→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   175→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   176→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   177→                    if issues > 1:
   178→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   179→                        if bulk > 0:
   180→                            print(colorize(
   181→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   182→                                "cyan",
   183→                            ))
   184→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   185→            log(f"  score impact estimate skipped: {exc}")
   186→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   187→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   188→        try:
   189→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   190→            if dim_key:
   191→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   192→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   193→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   194→                        continue
   195→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   196→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   197→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   198→                        if drag > 0.01:
   199→                            print(colorize(
   200→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   201→                                "cyan",
   202→                            ))
   203→                        break
   204→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   205→            log(f"  dimension drag estimate skipped: {exc}")
   206→
   207→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   208→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   209→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   210→        similar_count = sum(
   211→            1
   212→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   213→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   214→        )
   215→        if similar_count > 1:
   216→            print(
   217→                colorize(
   218→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   219→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   220→                    "cyan",
   221→                )
   222→            )
   223→    if explain:
   224→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   225→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   226→        base = (
   227→            f"ranked by confidence={confidence}, "
   228→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   232→        if dim_scores and detector:
   233→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   234→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   235→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   236→                base += (

   238→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   239→                )
   240→
   241→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   242→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   243→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   244→            if dim_key:
   245→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   246→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   247→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   248→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   249→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   250→                        break
   251→
   252→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   253→        if policy:
   254→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   255→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   256→
   257→
   258→def render_terminal_items(
   259→    items: list[dict],
   260→    dim_scores: dict,
   261→    findings_scoped: dict,
   262→    *,
   263→    group: str,
   264→    explain: bool,
   265→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   266→    plan: dict | None = None,
   267→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   268→) -> None:
   269→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   270→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   271→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   272→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   273→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   274→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   275→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   276→
   277→    if group != "item":
   278→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   279→        return
   280→
   281→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   282→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   283→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   284→    )
   285→
   286→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   287→        if idx > 0:
   288→            print()
   289→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   290→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   291→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   292→            continue
   293→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   294→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   295→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   296→        elif len(items) > 1:
   297→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   298→        else:
   299→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   300→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   301→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   302→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   303→
   304→
   305→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   306→    if len(items) != 1:
   307→        return
   308→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "finding")
   309→    if kind in ("cluster", "workflow_stage", "workflow_action"):
   310→        return  # These kinds have their own resolution hints
   311→    if kind != "finding":
   312→        return
   313→    item = items[0]
   314→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   315→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   316→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   317→        primary = item.get(
   318→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   319→        )
   320→        print(f"    {primary}")
   321→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   322→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   323→        return
   324→
   325→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   326→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   327→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   328→        print(f"    {primary}")
   329→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   330→    else:
   331→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   332→
   333→    print(
   334→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   335→    )
   336→    print(
   337→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   338→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   339→    )
   340→
   341→
   342→def render_followup_nudges(
   343→    state: dict,
   344→    dim_scores: dict,
   345→    findings_scoped: dict,
   346→    *,
   347→    strict_score: float | None,
   348→    target_strict_score: float,
   349→    queue_total: int = 0,
   350→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   351→    breakdown: "QueueBreakdown | None" = None,
   352→) -> None:
   353→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   354→        format_queue_block,
   355→    )
   356→
   357→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   358→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   359→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   360→        state,
   361→        subjective_entries,
   362→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   363→        max_quality_items=3,
   364→        max_integrity_items=5,
   365→    )
   366→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   367→    # Show frozen plan-start score + queue block when in an active cycle
   368→    if queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None and breakdown is not None:
   369→        frozen = plan_start_strict
   370→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen)
   371→        print()
   372→        for text, style in block:
   373→            print(colorize(text, style))
   374→        print(colorize(
   375→            "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   376→            "dim",
   377→        ))
   378→    elif queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None:
   379→        print(
   380→            colorize(
   381→                f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100",
   382→                "cyan",
   383→            )
   384→        )
   385→        print(
   386→            colorize(
   387→                f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   388→                " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   389→                "dim",
   390→            )
   391→        )
   392→    elif strict_score is not None:
   393→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   394→        if gap > 0:
   395→            print(
   396→                colorize(
   397→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   398→                    "cyan",
   399→                )
   400→            )
   401→        else:
   402→            print(
   403→                colorize(
   404→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   405→                    "green",
   406→                )
   407→            )
   408→    # Show queue block after north star when no frozen score
   409→    if breakdown is not None and queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is None:
   410→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   411→        for text, style in block:
   412→            print(colorize(text, style))
   413→
   414→    # Subjective bottleneck banner — only shown when the objective queue is
   415→    # clear.  While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority
   416→    # on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice.
   417→    _objective_remaining = max(
   418→        0,
   419→        (breakdown.queue_total - breakdown.subjective) if breakdown else queue_total,
   420→    )
   421→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores and _objective_remaining <= 0:
   422→        try:
   423→            health_breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   424→            subjective_drag = sum(
   425→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   426→                for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   427→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   428→            )
   429→            mechanical_drag = sum(
   430→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   431→                for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   432→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   433→            )
   434→            if subjective_drag > mechanical_drag and subjective_drag > 5.0:
   435→                print(colorize(
   436→                    f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   437→                    f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   438→                    "yellow",
   439→                ))
   440→                print(colorize(
   441→                    "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   442→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   443→                    "to re-score.",
   444→                    "yellow",
   445→                ))
   446→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   447→            log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   448→
   449→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   450→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   451→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   452→
   453→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   454→    if queue_total > 10:
   455→        print(colorize(
   456→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   457→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   458→            "dim",
   459→        ))
   460→
   461→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   462→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   463→        findings_scoped
   464→    )
   465→    parts: list[str] = []
   466→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   467→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   468→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   469→    open_review = [
   470→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   471→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   472→    ]
   473→    if low_dims:
   474→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   475→    if stale_count:
   476→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   477→    if unassessed_count:
   478→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   479→    if len(open_review):
   480→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   481→    if coverage_open > 0:
   482→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   483→
   484→    if parts:
   485→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   486→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   487→
   488→
   489→__all__ = [
   490→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   491→    "render_followup_nudges",
   492→    "render_gate_banner",
   493→    "render_queue_header",
   494→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   495→    "render_terminal_items",
   496→    "scorecard_subjective",
   497→    "show_empty_queue",
   498→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   499→]
   500→

    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    19→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    20→        "file": member.get("file"),
    21→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    22→        "status": member.get("status"),
    23→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    24→    }
    25→
    26→
    27→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    28→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    29→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    30→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    31→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    32→        serialized_members = [
    33→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    34→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    35→        ]
    36→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    37→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    38→            "id": item.get("id"),
    39→            "kind": "cluster",
    40→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    41→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    42→            "member_count": member_count,
    43→            "members": serialized_members,
    44→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    45→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    46→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    47→        }
    48→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    52→        return serialized_cluster
    53→
    54→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    55→        "id": item.get("id"),

    61→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    62→        "status": item.get("status"),
    63→    }
    64→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    65→
    66→    # Workflow dependency state
    67→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    68→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    69→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    70→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    71→
    72→    explain = item.get("explain")
    73→    if explain is not None:
    74→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    75→
    76→    # Plan metadata
    77→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    78→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    79→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    80→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    81→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    82→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    83→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    84→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    85→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    86→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    87→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    88→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    89→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    90→
    91→    return serialized
    92→
    93→
    94→def build_query_payload(
    95→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    96→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    97→    *,
    98→    command: str,
    99→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   100→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   101→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   102→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   103→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   104→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   105→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   106→        "suppressed_count": queue.get("suppressed_count", 0),
   107→    }
   108→
   109→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   110→        "command": command,
   111→        "items": serialized,
   112→        "queue": queue_section,
   113→        "narrative": narrative,
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    if plan and (
   117→        plan.get("queue_order")
   118→        or plan.get("skipped")
   119→        or plan.get("clusters")
   120→    ):
   121→        clusters_summary = []
   122→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   123→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   124→            clusters_summary.append({
   125→                "name": name,
   126→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   127→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   128→            })
   129→        payload["plan"] = {
   130→            "active": True,
   131→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   132→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   133→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   134→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   135→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   136→        }
   137→
   138→    return payload
   139→
   140→
   141→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   142→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   143→    lines = [
   144→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   145→        "",
   146→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   147→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   148→    ]
   149→    for item in items:
   150→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   151→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   152→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   153→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   154→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   155→    lines.append("")
   156→    return "\n".join(lines)
   157→
   158→
   159→def write_output_file(
   160→    output_file: str,
   161→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   162→    item_count: int,
   163→    *,
   164→    safe_write_text_fn,
   165→    colorize_fn,
   166→) -> bool:
   167→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   168→    try:
   169→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   170→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   171→    except OSError as exc:
   172→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   173→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   174→        return False
   175→    return True
   176→
   177→
   178→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   179→    output_format: str,
   180→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   181→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   182→) -> bool:
   183→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   184→    renderers = {
   185→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   186→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   187→    }
   188→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   189→    if renderer is None:
   190→        return False
   191→    renderer()
   192→    return True
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = [
   196→    "build_query_payload",
   197→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   198→    "render_markdown",
   199→    "serialize_item",
   200→    "write_output_file",
   201→]
   202→
```

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Maximise the **strict score** honestly. Your main cycle: **scan → plan → execute → rescan**. Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    19→
    20→**Don't be lazy.** Do large refactors and small detailed fixes with equal energy. If it takes touching 20 files, touch 20 files. If it's a one-line change, make it. No task is too big or too small — fix things properly, not minimally.
    21→
    22→## 2. The Workflow
    23→
    24→Three phases, repeated as a cycle.
    25→
    26→### Phase 1: Scan and review — understand the codebase
    27→
    28→```bash
    29→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    30→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    31→```
    32→
    33→The scan will tell you if subjective dimensions need review. Follow its instructions. To trigger a review manually:
    34→```bash
    35→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    36→```
    37→
    38→### Phase 2: Plan — decide what to work on
    39→
    40→After reviews, synthesis stages and plan creation appear as queue items in `next`. Complete them in order:
    41→```bash
    42→desloppify next                                        # shows the next workflow step
    43→desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "themes and root causes..."
    44→desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."
    45→desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "summary of priorities..."
    46→desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "execution plan..."
    47→```
    48→
    49→Then shape the queue. **The plan shapes everything `next` gives you** — don't skip this step.
    50→
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                          # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top           # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>    # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>          # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan skip <pat>              # defer — hide from next
    57→```
    58→
    59→More plan commands:
    60→```bash
    61→desloppify plan move <cluster> top       # move all cluster members at once
    62→desloppify plan move <a> <b> top         # mix clusters + findings in one move
    63→desloppify plan move <pat> before -t X   # position relative to another item/cluster
    64→desloppify plan cluster move a,b top     # move multiple clusters as one block
    65→desloppify plan done <pat>               # mark complete
    66→desloppify plan reopen <pat>             # reopen
    67→```
    68→
    69→### Phase 3: Execute — grind the queue to completion
    70→
    71→Trust the plan and execute. Don't rescan mid-queue — finish the queue first.
    72→
    73→```
    74→1. desloppify next              ← what to fix next
    75→2. Fix the issue in code
    76→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    77→4. Repeat until the queue is empty
    78→```
    79→
    80→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    81→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    82→
    83→**When the queue is clear, go back to Phase 1.** New issues will surface, cascades will have resolved, priorities will have shifted. This is the cycle.
    84→
    85→### Other useful commands
    86→
    87→```bash
    88→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    89→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    90→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    91→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    92→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" --attest "..." # accept debt
    93→desloppify exclude <path>                         # exclude a directory from scanning
    94→desloppify config show                            # show all config including excludes
    95→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    96→```
    97→
    98→## 3. Reference
    99→
   100→### How scoring works
   101→
   102→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
   103→
   104→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
   105→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
   106→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
   107→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
   108→
   109→### Subjective reviews in detail
   110→
   111→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   112→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   113→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   114→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   115→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   116→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   117→
   118→### Key concepts
   119→
   120→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   121→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   122→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   123→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   124→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   125→
   126→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   127→
   128→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   129→
   130→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   131→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   132→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   133→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   134→
   135→## Prerequisite
   136→
   137→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   138→
   139→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   140→
```
