```
   105→
   106→def show_focus_suggestion(
   107→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   108→) -> None:
   109→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   110→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   111→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   112→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   113→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   114→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   115→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   116→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   120→                "cyan",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        print()
   124→        return
   125→
   126→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   127→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   128→    if not lowest_name:
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   132→    lowest_kind = None
   133→    lowest_score = 101.0
   134→    lowest_issues = 0
   135→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   136→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   137→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   138→            if ds:
   139→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   140→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   141→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   142→            break
   143→    else:
   144→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   145→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   146→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   147→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   148→                lowest_issues = 0
   149→                break
   150→
   151→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   152→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   153→            print(
   154→                colorize(
   155→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   156→                    "cyan",
   157→                )
   158→            )
   159→            print()
   160→            return
   161→
   162→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   163→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   164→        if target_dim:
   165→            impact = 0.0
   166→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   167→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   168→                    {
   169→                        k: {
   170→                            "score": v["score"],
   171→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   172→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   173→                        }
   174→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   175→                        if "score" in v
   176→                    },
   177→                    potentials,
   178→                    det,
   179→                    lowest_issues,
   180→                )
   181→                if impact > 0:
   182→                    break
   183→
   184→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   185→            print(
   186→                colorize(
   187→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   188→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   189→                    "cyan",
   190→                )
   191→            )
   192→            print()
   193→

   274→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   275→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   276→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   277→    review_open = [
   278→        f
   279→        for f in findings.values()
   280→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   281→    ]
   282→    if not review_open:
   283→        return
   284→    uninvestigated = sum(
   285→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   286→    )
   287→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   288→    if uninvestigated:
   289→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   290→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   291→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   292→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   293→        print(
   294→            colorize(
   295→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   296→                "dim",
   297→            )
   298→        )
   299→    print()
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = [
   303→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    22→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    23→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    24→        plan_data = plan
    25→
    26→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    27→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    28→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    29→        if active_cluster:
    30→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    31→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    32→
    33→
    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    plan_data: dict | None,
    40→    new_count: int = 0,
    41→) -> None:
    42→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    43→        QueueBreakdown,
    44→        format_queue_headline,
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    total = len(items)
    48→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    49→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    50→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    51→
    52→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    58→    plan_ordered = 0
    59→    if plan_data:
    60→        queue_order = plan_data.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        skipped_ids = set(plan_data.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    62→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    63→
    64→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    65→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    66→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    67→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    68→        subjective=subjective,
    69→    )
    70→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    71→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    72→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    73→
    74→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    75→    if focus:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    77→
    78→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    79→        return
    80→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    81→        return
    82→    print(
    83→        colorize(
    84→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    85→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    92→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    93→        return items[:top]
    94→    return items
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    98→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    99→    _new = new_ids or set()
   100→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   101→        pos = str(idx)
   102→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   103→
   104→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   105→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   106→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   107→            conf_str = "—"
   108→            detector = "planning"
   109→            summary = f"{item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   110→            cluster_name = ""
   111→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   112→            conf_str = "—"
   113→            detector = "workflow"
   114→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   115→            cluster_name = ""
   116→        elif kind == "cluster":
   117→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   118→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   120→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   121→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   122→                if m.get("id") in _new
   123→            )
   124→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   125→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   126→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   127→        else:
   128→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   129→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   130→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   131→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   132→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   133→
   134→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   135→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   136→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   137→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   138→    return rows
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   149→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   150→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   151→
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   154→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   155→
   156→    queue = build_work_queue(
   157→        state,
   158→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   159→            count=None,
   160→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   161→            status="open",
   162→            include_subjective=True,
   163→            plan=plan_data,
   164→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   165→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   166→            collapse_clusters=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   170→
   171→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   172→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   173→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last synthesis)
   174→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   175→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   176→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   177→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   178→
   179→    if sort_by == "recent":
   180→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   181→
   182→    _print_queue_header(
   183→        items=items,
   184→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   185→        plan=plan,
   186→        plan_data=plan_data,
   187→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    if not items:
   191→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Determine which items to show
   195→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   196→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   197→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   198→
   199→    print()
   200→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   201→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   202→
   203→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   204→        remaining = len(items) - top
   205→        print(colorize(
   206→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   207→        ))
   208→    print()
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   212→
```

```
   119→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   120→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   121→
   122→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   123→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   124→    and v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue).
   125→    """
   126→    defaults = empty_plan()
   127→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   128→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   129→

   131→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   132→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   133→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   134→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   135→
   136→
   137→def synthesis_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   138→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   139→    return {

   142→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)

   150→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueueBreakdown:
    17→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    18→
    19→    queue_total: int = 0
    20→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    21→    skipped: int = 0
    22→    subjective: int = 0
    23→    workflow: int = 0
    24→    suppressed: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→    suppressed = int(result.get("suppressed_count", 0))
    50→
    51→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        suppressed=suppressed,
    98→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    99→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   100→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→
   108→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   109→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   110→
   111→    Examples::
   112→
   113→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   114→        Queue: 1934 items
   115→    """
   116→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   117→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   118→
   119→    # Parenthesized segments
   120→    segments: list[str] = []
   121→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   122→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   123→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   124→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   125→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   126→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   127→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   128→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   129→    if breakdown.suppressed > 0:
   130→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.suppressed} suppressed")
   131→
   132→    if segments:
   133→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   134→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   135→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   136→    return label
   137→
   138→
   139→def format_queue_block(
   140→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   141→    *,
   142→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   143→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   144→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   145→
   146→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   147→    """
   148→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   149→
   150→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   151→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   152→        focus_line = (
   153→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   154→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   155→            f" items remaining"
   156→        )
   157→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   158→
   159→    # Frozen score line
   160→    if frozen_score is not None:
   161→        lines.append((
   162→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   163→            "cyan",
   164→        ))
   165→
   166→    # Queue headline — always the same
   167→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   168→
   169→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   170→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   171→        lines.append((
   172→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   173→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   174→            "dim",
   175→        ))
   176→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   177→        lines.append((
   178→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   179→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   180→            "dim",
   181→        ))
   182→    else:
   183→        lines.append((
   184→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   185→            "dim",
   186→        ))
   187→
   188→    return lines
   189→
   190→
   191→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   192→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→
   195→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   196→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   197→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   198→
   199→    result = build_work_queue(
   200→        state,
   201→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   202→            status="open",
   203→            count=None,
   204→            plan=plan,
   205→            collapse_clusters=True,
   206→            include_skipped=False,
   207→        ),
   208→    )
   209→    return result["total"]
   210→
   211→
   212→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   213→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   214→    if not plan:
   215→        return None
   216→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   217→
   218→
   219→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   220→    plan: dict,
   221→    queue_remaining: int,
   222→    *,
   223→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   226→
   227→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   228→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   229→    """
   230→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   231→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   232→    if strict is None:
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    if breakdown is not None:
   236→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   237→        print()
   238→        for text, style in block:
   239→            print(colorize(text, style))
   240→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   241→            print(colorize(
   242→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   243→                "dim",
   244→            ))
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    # Legacy fallback
   248→    print(
   249→        colorize(
   250→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   251→            "cyan",
   252→        )
   253→    )
   254→    print(
   255→        colorize(
   256→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   257→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   258→            "dim",
   259→        )
   260→    )
   261→
   262→
   263→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   264→    state: dict,
   265→    prev,
   266→    plan: dict | None,
   267→) -> None:
   268→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   269→
   270→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
   271→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
   272→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
   273→    """
   274→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   275→        try:
   276→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   277→            remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   278→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   279→            breakdown = None
   280→            remaining = 0
   281→        if remaining > 0 and breakdown is not None:
   282→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   283→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   284→            )
   285→            return
   286→
   287→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
   288→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   289→
   290→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   291→
   292→
   293→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   294→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   295→
   296→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   297→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   298→    """
   299→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   300→
   301→    try:
   302→        plan = load_plan()
   303→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   304→        plan = None
   305→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   306→
   307→
   308→__all__ = [
   309→    "QueueBreakdown",
   310→    "format_queue_block",
   311→    "format_queue_headline",
   312→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   313→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   314→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   315→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   316→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   317→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   318→]
   319→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (

    27→)
    28→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    29→
    30→
    31→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    32→class QueueBuildOptions:
    33→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    34→
    35→    count: int | None = 1
    36→    scan_path: str | None = None
    37→    scope: str | None = None
    38→    status: str = "open"
    39→    include_subjective: bool = True
    40→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    41→    chronic: bool = False
    42→    explain: bool = False
    43→    plan: dict | None = None
    44→    include_skipped: bool = False
    45→    cluster: str | None = None
    46→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    47→
    48→
    49→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    50→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    51→
    52→    items: list[dict]
    53→    total: int
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→    suppressed_count: int
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    60→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    61→
    62→
    63→_WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
    85→            workflow_items.append(item)
    86→        elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    87→            subjective_items.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            objective_items.append(item)
    90→
    91→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    92→        return items
    93→
    94→    interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
    95→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:
   102→                break
   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
   104→
   105→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   106→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
   107→    return interleaved
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_work_queue(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   114→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   115→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   116→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   117→
   118→    status = resolved_options.status
   119→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   120→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   121→    try:
   122→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   123→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   124→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   125→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   126→
   127→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   128→        state,
   129→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   130→        status_filter=status,
   131→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   132→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   136→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   137→    suppressed_count = 0
   138→    if (
   139→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   140→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   141→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   142→    ):
   143→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   144→            state,
   145→            state.get("findings", {}),
   146→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   147→        )
   148→        for item in subjective_items:
   149→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   150→                continue
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    51→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    52→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    53→    weight_by_confidence = {
    54→        "high": 1.0,
    55→        "medium": 0.7,
    56→        "low": 0.3,
    57→    }
    58→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    59→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    60→        weight *= 10.0
    61→    return float(weight)
    62→
    63→
    64→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    65→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    66→    if not scope:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    70→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    71→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    72→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    73→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    75→
    76→    if "*" in scope:
    77→        return any(
    78→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    79→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    80→        )
    81→
    82→    if "::" in scope:
    83→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    84→
    85→    lowered = scope.lower()
    86→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        return (
    88→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    89→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    90→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    91→        )
    92→
    93→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    94→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    95→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    96→
    97→    return (
    98→        detector == scope
    99→        or filepath == scope
   100→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   105→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   106→
   107→
   108→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   109→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   110→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   111→    target = cleaned.lower()
   112→
   113→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   114→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   115→            return str(dim_key)
   116→    return slugify(cleaned)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   120→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   121→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   122→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   123→    return {
   124→        cleaned.lower(),
   125→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   126→        slugify(cleaned),
   127→        canonical.lower(),
   128→        slugify(canonical),
   129→    }
   130→
   131→
   132→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   133→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   134→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   135→    if not lang:
   136→        return None
   137→
   138→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   140→        return None
   141→
   142→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   143→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   144→        return None
   145→
   146→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   147→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   148→        return None
   149→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   150→
   151→
   152→def primary_command_for_finding(
   153→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   154→) -> str:
   155→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   156→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   158→        available_fixers = [
   159→            fixer
   160→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   161→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   162→        ]
   163→        if available_fixers:
   164→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   165→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   166→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   167→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   168→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   169→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   170→
   171→
   172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   174→
   175→    Returns an empty list when no synthesis stages are pending.
   176→    """
   177→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
   178→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   179→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
   180→    )
   181→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   182→        SYNTHESIS_IDS,
   183→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→
   195→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   196→    open_review_count = sum(
   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   199→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({
   224→            "id": sid,
   225→            "tier": 1,
   226→            "confidence": "high",
   227→            "detector": "synthesis",
   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   230→            "stage_name": name,
   231→            "stage_index": idx,
   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   233→            "detail": {
   234→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items
   243→
   244→
   245→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   246→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   247→
   248→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   249→    """
   250→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from collections import deque
     7→from typing import TypedDict
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,
    14→    build_synthesis_stage_items,
    15→    scope_matches,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    18→    build_finding_items,
    19→    group_queue_items,
    20→    item_explain,
    21→    item_sort_key,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    24→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    25→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    26→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    29→
    30→
    31→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    32→class QueueBuildOptions:
    33→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    34→
    35→    count: int | None = 1
    36→    scan_path: str | None = None
    37→    scope: str | None = None
    38→    status: str = "open"
    39→    include_subjective: bool = True
    40→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    41→    chronic: bool = False
    42→    explain: bool = False
    43→    plan: dict | None = None
    44→    include_skipped: bool = False
    45→    cluster: str | None = None
    46→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    47→
    48→
    49→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    50→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    51→
    52→    items: list[dict]
    53→    total: int
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→    suppressed_count: int
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    60→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    61→
    62→
    63→_WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
    85→            workflow_items.append(item)
    86→        elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    87→            subjective_items.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            objective_items.append(item)
    90→
    91→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    92→        return items
    93→
    94→    interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
    95→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:
   102→                break
   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
   104→
   105→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   106→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
   107→    return interleaved
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_work_queue(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   114→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   115→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   116→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   117→
   118→    status = resolved_options.status
   119→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   120→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   121→    try:
   122→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   123→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   124→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   125→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   126→
   127→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   128→        state,
   129→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   130→        status_filter=status,
   131→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   132→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   136→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   137→    suppressed_count = 0
   138→    if (
   139→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   140→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   141→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   142→    ):
   143→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   144→            state,
   145→            state.get("findings", {}),
   146→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   147→        )
   148→        for item in subjective_items:
   149→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   150→                continue
   151→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   152→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   153→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   154→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   155→                suppressed_count += 1
   156→                continue
   157→            all_items.append(item)
   158→
   159→    # Inject synthesis stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   160→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   161→        synth_items = build_synthesis_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   162→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   163→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   164→        if plan_item is not None:
   165→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   166→
   167→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   168→
   169→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   170→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   171→    if resolved_options.plan:
   172→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   173→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   174→            all_items,
   175→            resolved_options.plan,
   176→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   177→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   178→            new_ids=new_ids,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   182→    should_collapse = (
   183→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   184→        and resolved_options.plan
   185→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   186→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   187→    )
   188→    if should_collapse:
   189→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   190→
   191→    should_interleave = (
   192→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   193→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   194→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   195→        and not resolved_options.scope
   196→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   197→    )
   198→    if should_interleave:
   199→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   200→
   201→    total = len(all_items)
   202→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   203→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   204→
   205→    if resolved_options.explain:
   206→        for item in all_items:
   207→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   208→
   209→    return {
   210→        "items": all_items,
   211→        "total": total,
   212→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   213→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   214→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→__all__ = [
   219→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   220→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   221→    "WorkQueueResult",
   222→    "build_work_queue",
   223→    "group_queue_items",
   224→]
   225→
```

```
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}

    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:

    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True

    50→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    51→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    52→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    53→    weight_by_confidence = {
    54→        "high": 1.0,
    55→        "medium": 0.7,
    56→        "low": 0.3,
    57→    }
    58→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    59→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    60→        weight *= 10.0
    61→    return float(weight)
    62→

    64→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    65→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    66→    if not scope:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    70→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    71→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    72→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    73→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    75→
    76→    if "*" in scope:
    77→        return any(
    78→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    79→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    80→        )
    81→
    82→    if "::" in scope:

    86→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":

   163→        if available_fixers:

   165→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   166→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   167→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   168→        return "desloppify show subjective"

   172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.

   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→

   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"

   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({

   226→            "confidence": "high",

   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",

   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",

   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items

   297→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   298→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   299→
   300→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   301→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   302→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   303→    """
   304→    entries: list[dict] = []
   305→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   306→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   307→            continue
   308→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   309→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   310→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   311→            continue
   312→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   313→            continue
   314→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   315→        entries.append(
   316→            {
   317→                "name": name,
   318→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   319→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   320→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   321→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   322→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   323→                "placeholder": True,
   324→                "stale": False,
   325→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   326→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   327→            }
   328→        )
   329→    return entries
   330→
   331→
   332→def build_subjective_items(
   333→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   334→) -> list[dict]:
   335→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   336→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   337→    if not dim_scores:
   338→        return []
   339→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   340→
   341→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   342→
   343→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   344→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   345→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   346→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   347→        for e in subjective_entries
   348→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   349→    }
   350→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   351→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   352→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   353→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   354→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   355→
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   412→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   413→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   414→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   415→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   416→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   417→        if open_review > 0:
   418→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   419→        elif is_unassessed:
   420→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   421→        elif is_stale:
   422→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   423→                cli_keys,
   424→                force_review_rerun=True,
   425→            )
   426→        elif cli_keys:
   427→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   428→                cli_keys,
   429→                force_review_rerun=True,
   430→            )
   431→        else:
   432→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   433→                cli_keys,
   434→                force_review_rerun=True,
   435→            )
   436→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   437→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   438→        items.append(
   439→            {
   440→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   441→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   442→                "file": ".",
   443→                "confidence": "medium",
   444→                "summary": summary,
   445→                "detail": {
   446→                    "dimension_name": name,
   447→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   448→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   449→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   450→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   451→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   452→                },
   453→                "status": "open",
   454→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   455→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   456→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   457→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   458→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   459→            }
   460→        )
   461→    return items
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    21→
    22→SYNTHESIS_PREFIX = "synthesis::"
    23→SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS = (
    24→    "synthesis::observe",
    25→    "synthesis::reflect",
    26→    "synthesis::organize",
    27→    "synthesis::commit",
    28→)
    29→SYNTHESIS_IDS = set(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS)
    30→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("synthesis::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    33→
    34→
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→# Result dataclasses
    37→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    20→
    21→
    22→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    23→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    24→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    25→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    26→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    27→
    28→
    29→def build_finding_items(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    *,
    32→    scan_path: str | None,
    33→    status_filter: str,
    34→    scope: str | None,
    35→    chronic: bool,
    36→) -> list[dict]:
    37→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    38→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    39→    out: list[dict] = []
    40→
    41→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    45→            continue
    46→        if chronic and not (
    47→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    48→        ):
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        item = dict(finding)
    52→        item["id"] = finding_id
    53→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    54→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    55→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    56→        item["review_weight"] = (
    57→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    58→        )
    59→        subjective_score = None
    60→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    61→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    62→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    63→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    64→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    65→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    66→            )
    67→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    68→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    69→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    70→            item,
    71→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    72→        )
    73→
    74→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    75→            continue
    76→        out.append(item)
    77→
    78→    return out
    79→
    80→
    81→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    82→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    83→
    84→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    86→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    # Synthesis stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
    89→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
    90→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
    91→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
    92→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
    93→
    94→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
    95→    if kind == "workflow_action":
    96→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    97→
    98→    if kind == "cluster":
    99→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   100→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   101→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   102→        )
   103→        return (
   104→            0,
   105→            action_pri,
   106→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   111→        return (
   112→            1,
   113→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   114→            subjective_score_value(item),
   115→            item.get("id", ""),
   116→        )
   117→
   118→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   119→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   120→    return (
   121→        1,
   122→        0,
   123→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   124→        -review_weight,
   125→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   126→        item.get("id", ""),
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   131→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   132→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   133→        return {
   134→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   135→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   136→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   137→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   138→            "policy": "Synthesis stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   139→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   140→        }
   141→
   142→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   143→        return {
   144→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   145→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after synthesis stages, before findings.",
   146→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   147→        }
   148→
   149→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   150→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   151→        return {
   152→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   153→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   154→            "initial_review": initial,
   155→            "policy": (
   156→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   157→                if initial else
   158→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   159→            ),
   160→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   161→        }
   162→
   163→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   164→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   165→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   166→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   167→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   168→    if is_subjective:
   169→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   170→    elif is_review:
   171→        ranking_factors = [
   172→            "confidence asc",
   173→            "review_weight desc",
   174→            "count desc",
   175→            "id asc",
   176→        ]
   177→    else:
   178→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   179→    explain = {
   180→        "kind": "finding",
   181→        "confidence": confidence,
   182→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   183→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   184→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   185→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   186→    }
   187→    if is_review:
   188→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   189→    if is_subjective:
   190→        explain["policy"] = (
   191→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   192→        )
   193→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   194→    return explain
   195→
   196→
   197→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   198→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   199→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   200→    for item in items:
   201→        if group == "file":
   202→            key = item.get("file", "")
   203→        elif group == "detector":
   204→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   205→        elif group == "cluster":
   206→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   207→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   208→        else:
   209→            key = "items"
   210→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   211→    return grouped
   212→
   213→
   214→__all__ = [
   215→    "build_finding_items",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "subjective_score_value",
   219→    "group_queue_items",
   220→]
   221→
```

```
1→"""Shared synthesis guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     9→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    SYNTHESIS_IDS,
    13→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    14→    is_synthesis_stale,
    15→    load_plan,
    16→    synthesis_phase_banner,
    17→)
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    22→    """Structured result from synthesis staleness detection."""
    23→
    24→    is_stale: bool = False
    25→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    26→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    27→
    28→
    29→def synthesis_guardrail_status(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict | None = None,
    32→    state: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    34→    """Pure detection: is synthesis stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    35→    try:
    36→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    37→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    38→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult()
    39→
    40→    resolved_state = state or {}
    41→
    42→    if not is_synthesis_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    43→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    44→
    45→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    46→    if resolved_state:
    47→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    48→
    49→    return SynthesisGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    50→
    51→
    52→def print_synthesis_guardrail_info(
    53→    *,
    54→    plan: dict | None = None,
    55→    state: dict | None = None,
    56→) -> bool:
    57→    """Print yellow info banner if synthesis is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    58→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    59→    if not result.is_stale:
    60→        return False
    61→
    62→    if result.new_ids:
    63→        print(colorize(
    64→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    65→            "yellow",
    66→        ))
    67→
    68→    if result._plan is not None:
    69→        banner = synthesis_phase_banner(result._plan)
    70→        if banner:
    71→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    72→
    73→    return True
    74→
    75→
    76→def require_synthesis_current_or_exit(
    77→    *,
    78→    state: dict,
    79→    bypass: bool = False,
    80→    attest: str = "",
    81→) -> None:
    82→    """Gate: exit(1) if synthesis is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    83→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(state=state)
    84→    if not result.is_stale:
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    88→        print(colorize(
    89→            "  Synthesis guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    90→            "yellow",
    91→        ))
    92→        return
    93→
    94→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    95→    print(colorize(
    96→        f"\n  BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged.",
    97→        "red",
    98→    ))
    99→    if new_ids:
   100→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   101→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
   102→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}", "red"))
   103→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   105→    print()
   106→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan synthesize", "yellow"))
   107→    print(colorize(
   108→        "  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)",
   109→        "dim",
   110→    ))
   111→    print()
   112→    print(colorize("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   113→    print(colorize(
   114→        '  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."',
   115→        "dim",
   116→    ))
   117→    print()
   118→    sys.exit(1)
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "SynthesisGuardrailResult",
   123→    "print_synthesis_guardrail_info",
   124→    "require_synthesis_current_or_exit",
   125→    "synthesis_guardrail_status",
   126→]
   127→
```
