```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     7→
     8→
     9→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    10→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    11→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    12→    if not scan_history:
    13→        return set()
    14→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    15→    if not threshold:
    16→        return set()
    17→    return {
    18→        finding_id
    19→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    20→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def apply_plan_order(
    25→    items: list[dict],
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    29→    cluster: str | None = None,
    30→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    33→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    34→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    35→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    36→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    37→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    38→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    39→
    40→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for item in items:
    42→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    43→
    44→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    45→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    46→        if override.get("description"):
    47→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    48→        if override.get("note"):
    49→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    50→        if override.get("cluster"):
    51→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    52→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    53→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    54→                "name": cluster_name,
    55→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    56→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    57→            }
    58→
    59→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    60→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    61→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    62→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    63→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    64→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    65→
    66→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    68→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    69→    _new = new_ids or set()
    70→    for item in items:
    71→        item_id = item["id"]
    72→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    73→            continue
    74→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    75→            skipped_items.append(item)
    76→        elif item_id in _new:
    77→            remaining_new.append(item)
    78→        else:
    79→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    80→
    81→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    82→    if include_skipped:
    83→        result = result + skipped_items
    84→
    85→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
    86→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
    87→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    88→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
    89→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
    90→            if skip_entry:
    91→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
    92→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
    93→                if skip_reason:
    94→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    95→
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
    97→    if effective_cluster:
    98→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
    99→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   100→        if cluster_member_ids:
   101→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   102→    return result
   103→
   104→
   105→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   106→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   107→    try:
   108→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   109→
   110→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   111→        if meta:
   112→            return meta.action_type
   113→    except ImportError as exc:
   114→        _ = exc
   115→    return "manual_fix"
   116→
   117→
   118→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   119→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   120→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   121→    if not clusters:
   122→        return items
   123→
   124→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   125→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   126→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   127→            continue
   128→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   129→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   130→
   131→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   132→        return items
   133→
   134→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   135→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   136→    for item in items:
   137→        cluster_name = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   138→        if cluster_name:
   139→            cluster_members.setdefault(cluster_name, []).append(item)
   140→        else:
   141→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   142→
   143→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   144→    for cluster_name, members in cluster_members.items():
   145→        if len(members) < 2:
   146→            result.extend(members)
   147→            continue
   148→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   149→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   150→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   151→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   152→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   153→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   154→            action_type = "reorganize"
   155→        else:
   156→            action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   157→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   158→                action_type = "refactor"
   159→
   160→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   161→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   162→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   163→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   164→        else:
   165→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   166→
   167→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   168→        if not primary_command:
   169→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   170→
   171→        meta_item: dict = {
   172→            "id": cluster_name,
   173→            "kind": "cluster",
   174→            "action_type": action_type,
   175→            "summary": summary,
   176→            "members": members,
   177→            "member_count": len(members),
   178→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   179→            "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   180→            "cluster_auto": True,
   181→            "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   182→            "confidence": "high",
   183→            "detector": detector,
   184→            "file": "",
   185→        }
   186→        result.append(meta_item)
   187→
   188→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   189→    return result
   190→
   191→
   192→__all__ = ["apply_plan_order", "collapse_clusters", "new_item_ids"]
   193→
```

```
    91→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    92→    """Return a banner string when synthesis stage IDs are in the queue."""
    93→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    94→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    95→    has_synthesis = any(sid in order for sid in SYNTHESIS_IDS) or SYNTHESIS_ID in order

    97→        return ""
    98→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    99→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   100→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   101→    if completed:
   102→        return (
   103→            f"Planning mode in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
   104→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
   105→        )
   106→    return (
   107→        "Planning mode needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
   108→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
```

```
     2→
     3→Implements a staged workflow: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT
     4→with a fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING skip path.
     5→
     6→Stage gates validate **plan data enrichment**, not text reports:
     7→- OBSERVE: lightweight — write an analysis of themes/root causes (100 char min)
     8→- REFLECT: compare current findings against completed work (recurring patterns)
     9→- ORGANIZE: structural — each manual cluster must have description + action_steps
    10→- COMMIT: strategy must be substantive (200 char min) or "same"
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import argparse
    16→from collections import defaultdict

    37→    build_synthesis_prompt,
    38→    collect_synthesis_input,
    39→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    40→    extract_finding_citations,
    41→)
    42→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
    43→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    44→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    45→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    46→    SYNTHESIS_ID,
    47→    SYNTHESIS_IDS,

    49→    load_plan,

    51→    save_plan,

    53→
    54→
    55→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _has_synthesis_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Check if any synthesis stage ID (new or legacy) is in the queue."""
    60→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    61→    return bool(order & SYNTHESIS_IDS) or SYNTHESIS_ID in order
    62→
    63→
    64→def _inject_synthesis_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    65→    """Inject all 4 synthesis stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    66→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    67→    existing = set(order)
    68→    # Remove legacy ID if present
    69→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in existing:
    70→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
    71→    for sid in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS:
    72→        if sid not in existing:
    73→            order.insert(0 if sid == SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    74→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    75→    for sid in reversed(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS):
    76→        if sid in order:
    77→            order.remove(sid)
    78→    insert_at = 0
    79→    for sid in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS:
    80→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    81→        insert_at += 1
    82→
    83→
    84→def _purge_synthesis_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    85→    """Purge a single synthesis stage ID from the queue."""
    86→    sid = f"synthesis::{stage_name}"
    87→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    88→
    89→
    90→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    91→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    92→    try:
    93→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    94→    except ValueError:
    95→        return []
    96→    cleared: list[str] = []
    97→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    98→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    99→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
   100→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    21→
    22→SYNTHESIS_PREFIX = "synthesis::"
    23→SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS = (
    24→    "synthesis::observe",
    25→    "synthesis::reflect",
    26→    "synthesis::organize",
    27→    "synthesis::commit",
    28→)
    29→SYNTHESIS_IDS = set(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS)
    30→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("synthesis::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    33→
    34→
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→# Result dataclasses
    37→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
```

```
151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   161→
   162→    # Remove from skipped if present
   163→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   164→    for fid in finding_ids:
   165→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   166→
   167→    # Remove from current position in order
   168→    for fid in finding_ids:
   169→        if fid in order:
   170→            order.remove(fid)
   171→
   172→    # Resolve insertion point
   173→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   174→
   175→    # Insert in original order
   176→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   177→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   178→
   179→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   180→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   181→    if position == "top":
   182→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   183→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   184→        for fid in finding_ids:
   185→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   186→                promoted.append(fid)
   187→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   188→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   189→
   190→    return len(finding_ids)
   191→
   192→
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→# Skip / unskip
   195→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   196→
   197→def skip_items(
   198→    plan: PlanModel,
   199→    finding_ids: list[str],
   200→    *,
   201→    kind: str = "temporary",
   202→    reason: str | None = None,
   203→    note: str | None = None,
   204→    attestation: str | None = None,
   205→    review_after: int | None = None,
   206→    scan_count: int = 0,
   207→) -> int:
   208→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   209→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   210→    now = utc_now()
   211→    count = 0
   212→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   213→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   214→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   215→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   216→    for fid in finding_ids:
   217→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   218→        skipped[fid] = {
   219→            "finding_id": fid,
   220→            "kind": kind,
   221→            "reason": reason,
   222→            "note": note,
   223→            "attestation": attestation,
   224→            "created_at": now,
   225→            "review_after": review_after,
   226→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   227→        }
   228→        count += 1
   229→    return count
   230→
   231→
   232→def unskip_items(
   233→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   234→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   235→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   236→
   237→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   238→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   239→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   240→    """
   241→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   242→    count = 0
   243→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   244→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   245→    for fid in finding_ids:
   246→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   247→        if entry is not None:
   248→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   249→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   250→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
```

```
1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel

     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    38→        QueueBreakdown,
    39→        format_queue_headline,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    43→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    44→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    45→    skipped = len(skipped_ids)
    46→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    48→
    49→    # Build a lightweight breakdown for the headline
    50→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    51→        queue_total=ordered,
    52→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    53→        skipped=skipped,
    54→    )
    55→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    56→
    57→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    58→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {headline}", "dim"))
    59→    if active:
    60→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    61→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    62→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    63→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    64→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    65→
    66→
    67→def print_replacement_groups(
    68→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    69→    *,
    70→    title: str,
    71→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    72→) -> None:
    73→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    74→    if not groups:
    75→        return
    76→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    77→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    78→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    79→        for old, new in replacements:
    80→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    81→    print()
    82→
    83→
    84→def print_ranked_actions(
    85→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    86→) -> bool:
    87→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    88→    ranked = sorted(
    89→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    90→        key=lambda action: (
    91→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    92→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    93→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    94→        ),
    95→    )
    96→    if not ranked:
    97→        return False
    98→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    99→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
   100→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
   101→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
   102→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
   103→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
   104→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
   105→        if cluster_count:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
   107→        else:
   108→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
   109→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
   110→    return True
   111→
   112→
   113→__all__ = [
   114→    "print_agent_plan",
   115→    "print_ranked_actions",
   116→    "print_replacement_groups",
   117→]
```

```
24→def apply_plan_order(
    25→    items: list[dict],
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    29→    cluster: str | None = None,
    30→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    33→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    34→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    35→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    36→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    37→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    38→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    39→
    40→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for item in items:
    42→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    43→
    44→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    45→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    46→        if override.get("description"):
    47→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    48→        if override.get("note"):
    49→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    50→        if override.get("cluster"):
    51→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    52→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    53→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    54→                "name": cluster_name,
    55→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    56→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    57→            }
    58→
    59→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    60→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    61→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    62→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    63→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    64→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    65→
    66→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    68→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    69→    _new = new_ids or set()
    70→    for item in items:
    71→        item_id = item["id"]
    72→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    73→            continue
    74→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    75→            skipped_items.append(item)
    76→        elif item_id in _new:
    77→            remaining_new.append(item)
    78→        else:
    79→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    80→
    81→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    82→    if include_skipped:
    83→        result = result + skipped_items
    84→
    85→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
    86→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
    87→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    88→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
    89→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
    90→            if skip_entry:
    91→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
    92→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
    93→                if skip_reason:
    94→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    95→
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
    97→    if effective_cluster:
    98→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
    99→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   100→        if cluster_member_ids:
   101→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   102→    return result
   103→
```

```
63→_WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
    85→            workflow_items.append(item)
    86→        elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    87→            subjective_items.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            objective_items.append(item)
    90→
    91→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    92→        return items
    93→
    94→    interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
    95→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:
   102→                break
   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
   104→
   105→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   106→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
   107→    return interleaved
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_work_queue(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
```

```
def test_blocked_by_chain(self):
        plan = empty_plan()
        plan["queue_order"] = list(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS)
        state = _state_with_review_findings("r1")
        items = build_synthesis_stage_items(plan, state)
        # observe has no dependencies
        assert items[0]["blocked_by"] == []
        assert not items[0]["is_blocked"]
        # reflect depends on observe
        assert "synthesis::observe" in items[1]["blocked_by"]
        assert items[1]["is_blocked"]

    def test_skips_confirmed_stages(self):
        plan = empty_plan()
        plan["queue_order"] = list(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS)
        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
            "synthesis_stages": {"observe": {"report": "done"}},
        }
        state = _state_with_review_findings("r1")
        items = build_synthesis_stage_items(plan, state)
        ids = [it["id"] for it in items]
        assert "synthesis::observe" not in ids
        assert "synthesis::reflect" in ids
```

```
# ── Workflow items bypass interleave guardrail ─────────────


def test_workflow_items_preserved_at_front_during_interleave():
    """Workflow items (workflow_stage, workflow_action) should not participate
    in the subjective/objective interleave — they stay at the front."""
    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail

    workflow = {"id": "synthesis::observe", "kind": "workflow_stage"}
    obj1 = {"id": "smells::a", "kind": "finding"}
    obj2 = {"id": "smells::b", "kind": "finding"}
    obj3 = {"id": "smells::c", "kind": "finding"}
    subj1 = {"id": "subjective::naming", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
    subj2 = {"id": "subjective::clarity", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}

    items = [workflow, obj1, obj2, obj3, subj1, subj2]
    result = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(items)

    # Workflow item must be first
    assert result[0]["id"] == "synthesis::observe"
    # All items preserved
    assert len(result) == 6
    # No workflow items mixed into the interleaved portion
    result_ids = [r["id"] for r in result]
    assert result_ids.index("synthesis::observe") == 0


def test_workflow_action_also_bypasses_interleave():
    """workflow_action kind is also excluded from interleave."""
    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail

    wf = {"id": "workflow::create-plan", "kind": "workflow_action"}
    obj = {"id": "smells::a", "kind": "finding"}
    subj = {"id": "subjective::naming", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
```

```
def test_serialize_item_includes_blocked_by_for_workflow_stage():
    """serialize_item includes blocked_by and is_blocked for workflow_stage items."""
    item = {
        "id": "synthesis::reflect",
        "kind": "workflow_stage",
        "confidence": "high",
        "detector": "synthesis",
        "file": ".",
        "summary": "Planning: reflect",
        "status": "open",
        "blocked_by": ["synthesis::observe"],
        "is_blocked": True,
    }
    result = next_output.serialize_item(item)
    assert result["blocked_by"] == ["synthesis::observe"]
    assert result["is_blocked"] is True
```

### **Core Item Kinds Used**

The codebase uses two internal item kinds to represent planning mode:
- **`workflow_stage`**: Synthesis stages (observe, reflect, organize, commit)
- **`workflow_action`**: Workflow items like create-plan

These are filtered together as `_WORKFLOW_KINDS` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` line 63.

---

### **Key Insights for Discoverability Improvements**

1. **"Planning stage"** and **"Planning:"** are the main identifiers for synthesis in next/queue views
2. **"planning step"** appears in queue headlines when synthesis is pending
3. **"Planning mode"** appears in guardrail banners but may not be visible on every CLI surface
4. The distinction between synthesis (analysis/planning) and execution is communicated via queue item kinds
5. Queue counts explicitly show planning steps separately from other categories

```
172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   174→
   175→    Returns an empty list when no synthesis stages are pending.
   176→    """
   177→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
   178→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   179→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
   180→    )
   181→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   182→        SYNTHESIS_IDS,
   183→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→
   195→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   196→    open_review_count = sum(
   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   199→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({
   224→            "id": sid,
   225→            "tier": 1,
   226→            "confidence": "high",
   227→            "detector": "synthesis",
   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   230→            "stage_name": name,
   231→            "stage_index": idx,
   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   233→            "detail": {
   234→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items
   243→
```

## Analysis of the Blocked Synthesis Stage Ordering Issue

Based on my investigation of the desloppify codebase, I've identified the exact problem causing `synthesis::commit` (blocked) to appear as #1 instead of `synthesis::observe` (unblocked). Here's the complete breakdown:

### Problem Statement

When `next` is run with synthesis stages in the queue, it shows a blocked stage (e.g., `synthesis::commit`) as the top item instead of the first unblocked stage (`synthesis::observe`). The user expects the unblocked stage to be #1.

### Root Cause Analysis

The issue stems from **how `stage_index` is computed in `build_synthesis_stage_items()`** combined with **how items are sorted by `item_sort_key()`**.

#### Key Code Sections:

1. **In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` lines 206-231:**
   ```python
   for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
       if sid not in present:
           continue  # Skip if not in queue
       if name in confirmed:
           continue  # Skip if already completed
       
       items.append({
           ...
           "stage_index": idx,  # BUG: idx is the global position (0, 1, 2, 3)
           "blocked_by": blocked_by,
           "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
       })
   ```

   **The Problem:** When iterating through `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS = ("synthesis::observe", "synthesis::reflect", "synthesis::organize", "synthesis::commit")`, the `idx` variable tracks the global position in this tuple. However, when some stages are missing or already confirmed, the `stage_index` is still set to the global index, not the relative position among **present** items.

   **Example Scenario:**
   - Suppose the queue has: `["synthesis::organize", "synthesis::commit"]` (observe and reflect already done)
   - When building items:
     - "organize": `idx=2` (third in full list), `is_blocked=False` (reflect is confirmed)
     - "commit": `idx=3` (fourth in full list), `is_blocked=True` (organize not confirmed yet)

2. **In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` lines 89-92:**
   ```python
   if kind == "workflow_stage":
       blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
       stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
       return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   ```

   **Sort Key Tuple:** `(-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, id)`
   - This means within workflow_stage items:
     - Lower `blocked_penalty` (0) comes before higher (1)
     - If `blocked_penalty` is equal, lower `stage_index` comes first

   **The Sorting Issue:** If `synthesis::commit` has `stage_index=3` and `is_blocked=True`, its sort key is `(-2, 1, 3, "synthesis::commit")`. If `synthesis::organize` has `stage_index=2` and `is_blocked=False`, its key is `(-2, 0, 2, "synthesis::organize")`. The `0` beats `1` on the second tuple element, so organize should come first—which is correct.

   **However**, the real problem is when stages are **confirmed/removed** from iteration:

3. **The Real Issue - Stage Index Gap:**

   When `build_synthesis_stage_items()` skips confirmed stages with `continue`, the `stage_index` values become non-sequential in the returned list. For example:
   
   - `synthesis::observe`: idx=0, confirmed → skipped (not in items list)
   - `synthesis::reflect`: idx=1, confirmed → skipped (not in items list)  
   - `synthesis::organize`: idx=2, present, not confirmed → added with `stage_index=2`
   - `synthesis::commit`: idx=3, present, not confirmed → added with `stage_index=3`, `blocked_by=["synthesis::organize"]`

   The problem appears when stages are **reordered by `apply_plan_order()`** before `item_sort_key()` is applied, OR when stages are in the queue in **non-sequential order**.

### Pipeline Flow That Causes the Issue

Looking at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 159-199:

```python
1. all_items = list(finding_items)
2. all_items.extend(synth_items)              # Line 162: Synthesis items added
3. plan_item = build_create_plan_item(...)
4. if plan_item is not None:
       all_items.append(plan_item)            # Line 165: Workflow action added

5. all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)          # Line 167: INITIAL SORT
   # At this point, blocked synthesis items sort AFTER unblocked ones
   # because blocked_penalty=1 > 0

6. new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
7. all_items = _apply_plan_order(...)         # Line 173-179: REORDER per plan
   # This reorders items based on queue_order in the plan
   # The plan's queue_order might have synthesis stages in any order

8. should_interleave = ...                    # Line 191-197
9. if should_interleave:
       all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(...)  # Line 199
```

#### The Critical Problem

In **step 7** (`_apply_plan_order`), if the plan's `queue_order` lists synthesis stages in a different order than their natural dependency order, the items are reordered to match. 

Looking at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` lines 59-81:

```python
ordered: list[dict] = []
ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
for finding_id in queue_order:                    # Iterate plan's queue_order
    if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
        ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])        # Append in queue_order sequence
        ordered_ids.add(finding_id)

# ... later ...
result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
```

**The Problem:** If `queue_order` lists `["synthesis::commit", "synthesis::observe", ...]`, then `_apply_plan_order()` will place them in that order, regardless of `blocked_by` status or sort keys.

### Where Synthesis Stages Enter the Queue

In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py` lines 64-81:

```python
def _inject_synthesis_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    """Inject all 4 synthesis stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    existing = set(order)
    
    # Remove legacy ID if present
    if SYNTHESIS_ID in existing:
        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
    
    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    for sid in reversed(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS):
        if sid in order:
            order.remove(sid)
    
    insert_at = 0
    for sid in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS:
        order.insert(insert_at, sid)        # Insert at position 0, 1, 2, 3
        insert_at += 1
```

This function inserts stages in the correct order at the front of the queue. **So the queue_order should have the stages in the right order.**

However, there's another mechanism in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` that might reorder them.

### The Actual Root Cause

After careful review, **the issue is in the `stage_index` assignment logic in `build_synthesis_stage_items()`**:

The function builds items in the order that stages appear in `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS`, skipping confirmed ones. When it assigns `stage_index = idx`, it uses the loop counter which reflects position in the full SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS list, not the position among **returned items**.

**Concrete Example:**
- `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS = ("synthesis::observe", "synthesis::reflect", "synthesis::organize", "synthesis::commit")`
- Suppose: observe=done, reflect=done, organize and commit are pending
- Loop iteration:
  ```
  idx=0: "observe" - confirmed, skipped (continue)
  idx=1: "reflect" - confirmed, skipped (continue)
  idx=2: "organize" - not confirmed, added with stage_index=2, is_blocked=False
  idx=3: "commit" - not confirmed, added with stage_index=3, is_blocked=True
  ```
- Returned items list: `[organize_item, commit_item]`

Then in the sort key for commit: `(-2, 1, 3, "id")` (blocked_penalty=1, stage_index=3)
And for organize: `(-2, 0, 2, "id")` (blocked_penalty=0, stage_index=2)

The sort is: `(-2, 0, 2, ...) < (-2, 1, 3, ...)` → organize comes first ✓ This is correct!

**So the sort key is actually correct!**

### The True Problem: Plan Reordering

The real issue is that after the initial sort by `item_sort_key()`, the items are reordered by `_apply_plan_order()` according to the plan's `queue_order`. If `queue_order` has synthesis stages in the wrong order (perhaps due to manual moves with `plan move`), they will be displayed in that order, regardless of blocking status.

However, looking at `_inject_synthesis_stages()`, it carefully maintains order at the front of the queue.

### The Minimal Root Cause

After line-by-line analysis, I found the actual issue:

**In `build_synthesis_stage_items()` at line 206:**
```python
for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
```

When stages are skipped due to being confirmed, subsequent stages still get their index from the full tuple position. **But this alone shouldn't cause the problem** because the sort key has two components:
1. `blocked_penalty` (0 or 1) - primary sort
2. `stage_index` - secondary sort

**UNLESS** the `stage_index` values aren't being set correctly when building items in a partial/skipped scenario, and an item ends up with `stage_index=0` when it shouldn't.

Let me check if there's a case where `stage_index` could be wrong...

Actually, I see the issue now! When items are built:
- If observe is confirmed and skipped, the returned list won't have it
- Reflect is built with `idx=1`
- Organize is built with `idx=2`
- Commit is built with `idx=3`

But then they're in items list as: `[reflect, organize, commit]`

So Reflect's `stage_index=1` and is_blocked=True → sort key `(-2, 1, 1, ...)`
Organize's `stage_index=2` and is_blocked=False → sort key `(-2, 0, 2, ...)`

Comparison: `(-2, 1, 1) > (-2, 0, 2)` so organize comes first.

This is still correct!

**The real issue must be elsewhere.** Let me check if `_apply_plan_order()` is called AFTER the sort, which would reorder items:

Looking at core.py lines 167-179:
```python
all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)           # Line 167

if resolved_options.plan:
    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
    all_items = _apply_plan_order(...)      # Line 173: REORDERS items
```

**YES! This is it!** 

After `item_sort_key()` correctly sorts items with unblocked before blocked, `_apply_plan_order()` **completely reorders them** according to `plan["queue_order"]`.

If `plan["queue_order"]` has `synthesis::commit` before `synthesis::observe`, then `_apply_plan_order()` will place commit before observe, **completely ignoring the sort key ordering**.

### Summary: Root Cause

**The issue is NOT in the sort key logic.** The issue is that:

1. `item_sort_key()` correctly sorts synthesis stages: unblocked (blocked_penalty=0) before blocked (blocked_penalty=1)
2. But then `_apply_plan_order()` on line 173 of `core.py` **completely reorders** items based on `plan["queue_order"]`
3. If `plan["queue_order"]` has synthesis stages in a non-dependency order (e.g., due to a `plan move` command), the blocked stages can end up before unblocked ones

**The `apply_plan_order()` function does not respect the `is_blocked` flag** — it just uses the order from the plan.

### The Minimal Fix

There are two possible approaches:

**Option 1: Respect blocking status in `apply_plan_order()`**
- After reordering per queue_order, re-sort synthesis stages (workflow_stage items) by their blocking status
- This ensures unblocked stages always come before blocked ones, regardless of queue_order

**Option 2: Prevent synthesis stages from being moved via plan move**
- Make `move_items()` in `operations.py` reject moves of synthesis stages with validation
- This prevents the queue_order from getting out of dependency order

**Option 3: Preserve sort within `apply_plan_order()`**
- Group items by kind and within synthesis stages, maintain blocked/unblocked ordering

The **minimal and safest fix** is **Option 1**: After `apply_plan_order()` reorders items, resort just the synthesis stages to respect blocking status.

## Final Report

I've completed the investigation of the blocked synthesis stage ordering issue in the desloppify work queue pipeline. Here are my findings:

### Problem
The `next` command displays `synthesis::commit` (blocked) as item #1 instead of `synthesis::observe` (unblocked), despite the sort logic being correct.

### Root Cause
The issue occurs in the pipeline at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 167-179:

1. **Line 167:** `all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)` correctly sorts synthesis stages with unblocked items (blocked_penalty=0) before blocked items (blocked_penalty=1)

2. **Lines 173-179:** `_apply_plan_order()` then **completely reorders** all items based on `plan["queue_order"]`, ignoring the blocking status

If `plan["queue_order"]` contains synthesis stages in non-dependency order (possibly from a `plan move` command), blocked stages will be placed before unblocked ones, overriding the sort key logic.

### Key Code Locations

**Sort Key Logic** (correct):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` lines 89-92:
  ```python
  if kind == "workflow_stage":
      blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
      stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
      return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
  ```
  This puts unblocked (penalty=0) before blocked (penalty=1).

**Synthesis Item Building** (correct):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` lines 172-242:
  - Sets `blocked_by` and `is_blocked` based on `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES`
  - Dependencies defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis_playbook.py`:
    ```python
    SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES = {
        "observe": set(),
        "reflect": {"observe"},
        "organize": {"reflect"},
        "commit": {"organize"},
    }
    ```

**Order Reapplication** (problematic):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` lines 59-81:
  - `_apply_plan_order()` places items in `queue_order` sequence without respecting blocking

**Synthesis Injection** (creates order):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py` lines 64-81:
  - `_inject_synthesis_stages()` inserts stages in correct dependency order at the front

**Synthesis Stage Moves** (causes wrong order):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py` lines 151-190:
  - `move_items()` allows synthesis stages to be reordered, potentially violating dependencies

### The Interleave Guardrail (Not the Problem)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 66-107:
  - `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()` correctly **excludes** workflow items from interleaving and prepends them
  - This is not the issue

### Minimal Fix Approaches

**Option 1 (Recommended):** Re-sort synthesis stages in `apply_plan_order()` after reordering
- Ensure workflow_stage items maintain unblocked-before-blocked ordering post-reorder
- Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` line ~102 (after `result` is assembled)
- Add: Sort only items with `kind == "workflow_stage"` by their blocking status

**Option 2:** Validate/prevent synthesis stage moves
- Reject moves that would violate dependency order in `move_items()` 
- Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py` line ~173
- Add validation in `_resolve_position()` to detect synthesis stage reordering

**Option 3:** Inject synthesis stages after plan ordering
- Move synthesis item injection to happen after `_apply_plan_order()` rather than before
- This would preserve initial sort order throughout reordering

Option 1 is the minimal and safest fix that doesn't change the overall architecture.
